["ph_bluekasa"]={r6=false,hat=true,h={{n="Head",cf={7,0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1},s={2,1.2,1.2,1.2},k={{c="MD",n="PulseHat",k={{c="PT",r={7,0.0131,0.6734,0.0966,0,0,-1,-0.1015,0.9948,0,0.9948,0.1015,0},p={Size={2,1,1,1},Color={1,0.6392,0.6353,0.6471},Material={8,"Material","Plastic"},Transparency=0,Reflectance=0},k={{c="SH",p={MeshType={8,"MeshType","FileMesh"},MeshId="rbxassetid://13464094131",TextureId="rbxassetid://13463190504",Scale={2,1,1,1},Offset={2,0,0,0},VertexColor={2,1,1,1}}}}}}}}}}},
["ph_veil"]={r6=false,hat=true,h={{n="Head",cf={7,0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1},s={2,1.2,1.2,1.2},k={{c="MD",n="PulseHat",k={{c="PT",r={7,-0.0048,0.197,-0.1095,1,0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1},p={Size={2,2,2,2},Color={1,0.6392,0.6353,0.6471},Material={8,"Material","Plastic"},Transparency=0,Reflectance=0},k={{c="SH",p={MeshType={8,"MeshType","FileMesh"},MeshId="rbxassetid://5210039031",TextureId="rbxassetid://5210039113",Scale={2,1,1,1},Offset={2,0,0,0},VertexColor={2,1,1,1}}}}}}}}}}},
["ph_panda"]={r6=false,hat=true,h={{n="Head",cf={7,0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1},s={2,1.2,1.2,1.2},k={{c="MD",n="PulseHat",k={{c="PT",r={7,-0.0601,0.7996,0.0044,1,0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1},p={Size={2,2.2466,1.5182,2.1237},Color={1,0.6392,0.6353,0.6471},Material={8,"Material","Plastic"},Transparency=0,Reflectance=0},k={{c="SH",p={MeshType={8,"MeshType","FileMesh"},MeshId="rbxassetid://81279930936425",TextureId="rbxassetid://104542619736369",Scale={2,0.0225,0.0225,0.0225},Offset={2,0,0,0},VertexColor={2,1,1,1}}}}}}}}}}}
}
end

local OfflineEnvironment = {}

function OfflineEnvironment.create(host, hostGame)
	local hostGlobal = type(host.getgenv) == "function" and host.getgenv() or host
	local function lookup(name)
		local value = host[name]

		if value == nil then
			return hostGlobal[name]
		end

		return value
	end

	local environment = {}
	local diagnostics = {blocked = {}, kicks = {}, errors = {}}

	for name in string.gmatch([[
		assert error pcall xpcall pairs ipairs next select tonumber tostring type typeof
		rawget rawset rawequal rawlen setmetatable getmetatable unpack print warn buffer bit32 string table math
		coroutine vector os utf8 task wait spawn delay tick time elapsedTime gcinfo collectgarbage
		Enum Instance Color3 Vector2 Vector3 Vector2int16 Vector3int16 CFrame UDim UDim2 Rect Ray Region3 Region3int16
		BrickColor TweenInfo Font NumberRange NumberSequence NumberSequenceKeypoint ColorSequence ColorSequenceKeypoint
		PhysicalProperties RaycastParams OverlapParams Axes Faces DateTime Random CatalogSearchParams
		PathWaypoint FloatCurveKey RotationCurveKey Content Color3uint8 DockWidgetPluginGuiInfo
		Drawing gethui protectgui firetouchinterest fireclickdetector fireproximityprompt sethiddenproperty gethiddenproperty
		setclipboard toclipboard setfpscap getfpscap isnetworkowner getconnections getgc getsenv getscriptclosure
		getrawmetatable setrawmetatable setreadonly isreadonly checkcaller getnamecallmethod newcclosure iscclosure islclosure
		hookfunction hookmetamethod restorefunction mouse1press mouse1release mouse1click mouse2press mouse2release
		mouse2click mousemoverel mousemoveabs keypress keyrelease setthreadidentity getthreadidentity
		UserSettings settings version
	]], "%S+") do
		environment[name] = lookup(name)
	end

	environment._G = {}
	environment.shared = {}
	environment.getgenv = function() return environment end
	environment.getfenv = function() return environment end
	environment.getrenv = function() return environment end
	environment.setfenv = function(callback) return setfenv(callback, environment) end
	environment.identifyexecutor = function() return "PulseOffline", "1" end
	environment.is_from_loader = true
	environment.l_fastload_enabled = true

	local hostDebug = lookup("debug")
	environment.debug = {traceback = hostDebug.traceback, info = hostDebug.info}
	environment.debug.getinfo = function(callback)
		local source, line, name = hostDebug.info(callback, "sln")
		local count, vararg = hostDebug.info(callback, "a")

		return {
			source = source, short_src = source, currentline = line, name = name, func = callback,
			numparams = count, is_vararg = vararg, what = source == "[C]" and "C" or "Lua",
		}
	end

	local files = OfflineFiles.create({
		readfile = lookup("readfile"), writefile = lookup("writefile"), isfile = lookup("isfile"),
		makefolder = lookup("makefolder"), isfolder = lookup("isfolder"),
		getcustomasset = lookup("getcustomasset") or lookup("getsynasset"),
	})

	for name, callback in files do
		environment[name] = callback
	end
	environment.__pulseData = ResourceData.create(environment)

	local requestContext = {files = files, encodedFiles = OfflineFiles.resources, blocked = diagnostics.blocked}
	local function request(options)
		return OfflineRequests.request(requestContext, options)
	end

	environment.request = request
	environment.http_request = request
	environment.http = {request = request}
	environment.syn = {request = request, protect_gui = environment.protectgui, getcustomasset = files.getcustomasset}
	environment.WebSocket = {connect = function() error("WebSocket unavailable offline", 0) end}
	environment.websocket = environment.WebSocket

	local function queueOnTeleport(_source) end
	environment.queue_on_teleport = queueOnTeleport
	environment.queueonteleport = queueOnTeleport
	environment.syn.queue_on_teleport = queueOnTeleport
	environment.syn.queueonteleport = queueOnTeleport
	environment.fluxus = {queue_on_teleport = queueOnTeleport, queueonteleport = queueOnTeleport}

	local services = OfflineServices.create(hostGame, request, diagnostics)
	environment.game = services.game
	environment.__hostGame = hostGame
	environment.workspace = services.workspace
	environment.__offlineMethod = services.method
	environment.__offlineAddress = OfflineRequests.address
	environment.require = function(module)
		assert(typeof(module) == "Instance" and module:IsA("ModuleScript"), "External require disabled")
		return host.require(module)
	end

	environment.loadstring = function(source)
		if source == "return {" .. environment.readfile("PulseHub/cache/aura_data.98e13184.txt") .. "}" then
			return createAuraData
		end

		return nil, "Dynamic source unavailable offline"
	end
	environment.load = environment.loadstring

	return environment, diagnostics
end

local LocalResponseClient = {}

function LocalResponseClient.request(context, method, address, payload)
	local environment = context.environment

	if environment.type(context.request()) ~= "function" then
		return nil, "Executor has no HTTP requests"
	end

	local options = {
		Url = address, Method = method, Headers = {["Content-Type"] = "application/json"},
	}

	if payload then
		options.Body = context.json:JSONEncode(payload)
	end

	local ok, response = environment.pcall(context.request(), options)

	if not ok or environment.type(response) ~= "table" then
		return nil, "Request failed"
	end

	local code = response.StatusCode or response.status_code or 0
	local body = response.Body or response.body or ""

	environment.pcall(function()
		local headers = response.Headers or response.headers
		local encoding = environment.type(headers) == "table"
			and (headers["X-Pulse-Enc"] or headers["x-pulse-enc"] or headers["X-PULSE-ENC"])

		if not encoding or not context.decodePayload then
			return
		end

		local envelope = context.json:JSONDecode(body)

		if environment.type(envelope) ~= "table" or environment.type(envelope.e) ~= "string" then
			return
		end

		body = context.decodePayload(encoding, envelope.e)
	end)

	local data
	environment.pcall(function()
		data = context.json:JSONDecode(body)
	end)

	return {code = code, data = data}
end

local Music = {}

function Music.stop(environment, state)
	if state.sound then
		environment.pcall(function()
			state.sound:Stop()
			state.sound:Destroy()
		end)
		state.sound = nil
	end

	Music._destroy(environment, state, "firstResource")
	Music._destroy(environment, state, "secondResource")
	state.currentTrack = nil
	state.isPlaying = false
	environment.getgenv().__pulseMusicNow = nil

	environment.pcall(function()
		local stopRain = environment.getgenv().__pulseRainStop

		if stopRain then
			stopRain()
		end
	end)
end

function Music._destroy(environment, state, key)
	if not state[key] then
		return
	end

	environment.pcall(function()
		state[key]:Destroy()
	end)
	state[key] = nil
end

local TradeValues = {}
TradeValues.categories = {"godlies", "chromas", "ancients", "legendaries", "rares", "uncommons", "commons", "vintages", "misc", "pets"}
TradeValues.minimum = {godlies = 80, chromas = 30, commons = 150, uncommons = 100, rares = 80, legendaries = 40, ancients = 8, pets = 40}
TradeValues.rarities = {godlies = "Godly", ancients = "Ancient", legendaries = "Legendary", rares = "Rare", uncommons = "Uncommon", commons = "Common", vintages = "Classic"}

function TradeValues.normalize(value)
	return (string.lower(tostring(value or "")):gsub("[^%w]", ""))
end

function TradeValues.assetId(value)
	local id
	for match in tostring(value or ""):gmatch("%d%d%d%d%d+") do id = match end
	return id
end

function TradeValues.text(value)
	local text = tostring(value or "")
	local function codepoint(value, base)
		local number = tonumber(value, base)
		if not number or number >= 1114112 or number >= 0xD800 and number <= 0xDFFF then return "" end
		return utf8.char(number)
	end
	return (text:gsub("<[^>]+>", " "):gsub("&#(%d+);", function(value) return codepoint(value, 10) end)
		:gsub("&#[xX](%x+);", function(value) return codepoint(value, 16) end):gsub("&amp;", "&"):gsub("&quot;", '"')
		:gsub("&apos;", "'"):gsub("&nbsp;", " "):gsub("&lt;", "<"):gsub("&gt;", ">")
		:gsub("%s+", " "):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$"))
end

function TradeValues.parse(category, html, yieldTask)
	local result, cursor, scanned = {}, 1, 0
	while true do
		local first = html:find('<div class="itemcolumn"', cursor, true)
		if not first then break end
		local nextItem = html:find('<div class="itemcolumn"', first + 23, true)
		local item = html:sub(first, (nextItem or #html + 1) - 1)
		cursor = nextItem or #html + 1
		scanned += 1
		if yieldTask and scanned % 60 == 0 then yieldTask() end
		local value = tonumber(item:match('data%-value="([%d%.]+)"') or "")
		local name = item:match('<div class="itemhead">(.-)</div>')
		local origin = TradeValues.text(item:match('class="itemorigin">([^<]*)<') or "")
		if value and value < 500000 and name and not origin:find("Deletion", 1, true) then
			table.insert(result, {c = category, n = TradeValues.text(name), v = value, y = item:match('data%-year="(%d%d%d%d)"'),
				e = item:match('data%-event="([^"]+)"'), o = origin ~= "" and origin or nil,
				cl = item:match("Class %- <b>([^<]+)</b>"), dm = item:match('data%-demand="(%d+)"')})
		end
	end
	return result
end

function TradeValues.candidates(index, name, category, chroma)
	local result = {}
	local function eligible(entry)
		local item = entry.item
		if entry.kind == "p" then return (item.Chroma == true) == chroma and (category == "pets" or category == "chromas") end
		if category == "pets" then return false end
		if chroma then return item.Chroma == true end
		if item.Chroma then return false end
		if category == "misc" then return item.ItemType == "Misc" or item.Rarity == "Christmas" or item.Rarity == "Halloween" end
		return item.Rarity == TradeValues.rarities[category]
	end
	local prefix = category == "pets" and "pet:" or ""
	for _, entry in ipairs(index[prefix .. TradeValues.normalize(name)] or {}) do if eligible(entry) then table.insert(result, entry) end end
	if chroma and #result == 0 then
		for _, entry in ipairs(index["pet:" .. TradeValues.normalize(name)] or {}) do if eligible(entry) then table.insert(result, entry) end end
	end
	return result
end

function TradeValues.filter(entries, predicate)
	local filtered = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do if predicate(entry.item) then table.insert(filtered, entry) end end
	return #filtered > 0 and filtered or entries
end

function TradeValues.build(items, database, yieldTask)
	if type(database) ~= "table" or type(database.Item) ~= "table" then return nil end
	local weapons, pets = database.Item, type(database.Pets) == "table" and database.Pets or {}
	local index, petEntries = {}, {}
	local function addIndex(name, entry)
		index[name] = index[name] or {}
		table.insert(index[name], entry)
	end
	for id, item in pairs(weapons) do
		if type(item) ~= "table" then continue end
		local name = tostring(item.ItemName or item.Name or "")
		local entry = {kind = "i", id = tostring(id), item = item}
		addIndex(TradeValues.normalize(name), entry)
		local withoutYear = name:gsub("%s*%d%d%d%d%s*$", "")
		if withoutYear ~= name then addIndex(TradeValues.normalize(withoutYear), entry) end
	end
	for id, item in pairs(pets) do
		if type(item) ~= "table" then continue end
		local entry = {kind = "p", id = tostring(id), item = item}
		addIndex("pet:" .. TradeValues.normalize(item.Name), entry)
		table.insert(petEntries, entry)
	end
	local byKey, count = {}, 0
	for itemIndex, value in ipairs(items) do
		if yieldTask and itemIndex % 150 == 0 then yieldTask() end
		local category, amount = value.c, tonumber(value.v)
		if type(category) ~= "string" or not amount then continue end
		local name, kind = tostring(value.n or ""), nil
		local base, suffix = name:match("^(.-)%s*%((%a+)%)%s*$")
		if base and (suffix == "Knife" or suffix == "Gun") then name, kind = base, suffix end
		local year = value.y
		base, suffix = name:match("^(.-)%s*%((%d%d%d%d)%)%s*$")
		if base then name, year = base, suffix end
		if not year and value.o then year = tostring(value.o):match("(20%d%d)") end
		local chroma = category == "chromas"
		if chroma then name = name:gsub("^Chroma%s+", ""):gsub("^C%.%s+", "") end
		local aliases = {name}
		local wrapping = name:match("^(%a+) Papers$")
		if wrapping then table.insert(aliases, "Box of " .. wrapping .. " Wrapping Paper") end
		if name == "Ultra Wrap" then table.insert(aliases, "Box of Ultra Wrapping Paper") end
		if name:sub(-1) == "s" then table.insert(aliases, name:sub(1, -2)) end
		if category == "legendaries" then
			local short = name:match("^%S+%s+(.+)$")
			if short then table.insert(aliases, short) end
		end
		local matches = {}
		for _, alias in ipairs(aliases) do
			matches = TradeValues.candidates(index, alias, category, chroma)
			if #matches > 0 then break end
		end
		if category == "pets" and year then
			local correct = {}
			for _, entry in ipairs(matches) do if tostring(entry.item.Year or "") == tostring(year) then table.insert(correct, entry) end end
			if #correct == 0 then
				local prefix = TradeValues.normalize(name)
				for _, entry in ipairs(petEntries) do
					if TradeValues.normalize(entry.id):sub(1, #prefix) == prefix and tostring(entry.item.Year or "") == tostring(year) and not entry.item.Chroma then
						table.insert(correct, entry)
					end
				end
			end
			if #correct > 0 then matches = correct end
		end
		if kind then matches = TradeValues.filter(matches, function(item) return item.ItemType == kind end) end
		if #matches > 1 and value.cl then matches = TradeValues.filter(matches, function(item) return item.Rarity == value.cl end) end
		if #matches > 1 then
			local previous = matches
			if year then matches = TradeValues.filter(matches, function(item) return tostring(item.Year or "") == tostring(year) end) end
			if matches == previous then matches = TradeValues.filter(matches, function(item) return not item.Year end) end
		end
		if #matches > 1 then
			local previous = matches
			if value.e then matches = TradeValues.filter(matches, function(item) return tostring(item.Event or "") == value.e end) end
			if matches == previous then matches = TradeValues.filter(matches, function(item) return not item.Event end) end
		end
		for _, entry in ipairs(matches) do
			local key = entry.kind .. ":" .. entry.id
			local previous = byKey[key]
			if not previous then count += 1 end
			if not previous or previous.n >= #matches then byKey[key] = {v = amount, n = #matches} end
		end
	end
	local icon, chromaOf, byName = {}, {}, {}
	local function addIcon(item, key)
		local image = TradeValues.assetId(item.Image)
		if image and not icon[image] then icon[image] = key end
	end
	for id, item in pairs(weapons) do if type(item) == "table" then addIcon(item, "i:" .. tostring(id)) end end
	for id, item in pairs(weapons) do
		if type(item) == "table" and not item.Chroma and item.ItemID then
			local key = tostring(item.ItemID)
			if not icon[key] then icon[key] = "i:" .. tostring(id) end
		end
	end
	for id, item in pairs(pets) do if type(item) == "table" then addIcon(item, "p:" .. tostring(id)) end end
	for _, group in ipairs({{kind = "i", items = weapons, field = "ItemName"}, {kind = "p", items = pets, field = "Name"}}) do
		local regular = {}
		for id, item in pairs(group.items) do
			if type(item) ~= "table" or item.Chroma then continue end
			local key = TradeValues.normalize(item[group.field]) .. (group.kind == "i" and "|" .. tostring(item.ItemType) or "")
			if not regular[key] then regular[key] = group.kind .. ":" .. tostring(id) end
		end
		for id, item in pairs(group.items) do
			if type(item) ~= "table" then continue end
			local key = group.kind .. ":" .. tostring(id)
			local name = TradeValues.normalize(item[group.field])
			if item.Chroma then
				local base = regular[name .. (group.kind == "i" and "|" .. tostring(item.ItemType) or "")]
				if base then chromaOf[base] = key end
			end
			if byKey[key] then
				byName[name] = byName[name] or {}
				table.insert(byName[name], {key = key, chroma = item.Chroma == true})
			end
		end
	end
	return {byKey = byKey, icon = icon, chromaOf = chromaOf, byName = byName, n = count}
end

function TradeValues.format(value)
	if value >= 1000000 then return string.format("%.2fM", value / 1000000) end
	if value >= 10000 then return string.format("%.1fK", value / 1000) end
	if value >= 1000 then return string.format("%.2fK", value / 1000) end
	if value >= 100 then return tostring(math.floor(value + 0.5)) end
	if value >= 10 then return (string.format("%.1f", value):gsub("%.0$", "")) end
	return (string.format("%.2f", value):gsub("%.?0+$", ""))
end

function TradeValues.verdict(give, get)
	local difference = get - give
	if math.abs(difference) <= math.max(0.02 * math.max(give, get), 0.005) then return "even", nil end
	local sign = difference > 0 and "+" or "-"
	local percent = give > 0 and math.floor(math.abs(difference) / math.max(give, 0.0001) * 100 + 0.5) or nil
	return difference > 0 and "win" or "loss", sign .. TradeValues.format(math.abs(difference)) .. (percent and "  " .. sign .. math.min(percent, 99999) .. "%" or "")
end

function TradeValues.valueOf(state, tile, name)
	local mapped = state.mapped
	if not mapped then return nil end
	local tags = tile:FindFirstChild("Tags")
	local tag = tags and tags:FindFirstChild("Chroma")
	local chroma = tag ~= nil and tag.Visible == true
	local container = tile:FindFirstChild("Container")
	local icon = container and container:FindFirstChild("Icon")
	local id = icon and TradeValues.assetId(icon.Image)
	local key = id and mapped.icon[id]
	if key and chroma then key = mapped.chromaOf[key] or key end
	if key and mapped.byKey[key] then return mapped.byKey[key].v end
	for _, candidate in ipairs(mapped.byName[TradeValues.normalize(name)] or {}) do
		if candidate.chroma == chroma and mapped.byKey[candidate.key] then return mapped.byKey[candidate.key].v end
	end
	return nil
end

function TradeValues.database(state)
	local env = state.context.environment
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		local database = state.context.storage:FindFirstChild("Database")
		local module = database and database:FindFirstChild("Sync")
		if module then return env.require(module) end
	end)
	return ok and result or nil
end

function TradeValues.readCache(state)
	local context, env = state.context, state.context.environment
	local ok, value = pcall(function()
		if not env.isfile("PulseHub/sv_values.json") then return nil end
		return context.http:JSONDecode(env.readfile("PulseHub/sv_values.json"))
	end)
	if not ok or type(value) ~= "table" or type(value.items) ~= "table" or not tonumber(value.t) then return nil end
	return value
end

function TradeValues.fetch(state)
	local env = state.context.environment
	local values = {}
	for _, category in ipairs(TradeValues.categories) do
		if not state.context.isAlive() then return nil end
		local url = "offline://supremevalues.com/mm2/" .. category
		local ok, html = pcall(function() return env.game:HttpGet(url) end)
		if not ok or type(html) ~= "string" or #html <= 5000 or not html:find("itemcolumn", 1, true) then
			local success, response = pcall(env.request, {Url = url, Method = "GET", Headers = {Accept = "text/html"}})
			html = success and type(response) == "table" and (response.Body or response.body) or nil
		end
		if type(html) ~= "string" or #html <= 5000 or not html:find("itemcolumn", 1, true) then return nil end
		local entries = TradeValues.parse(category, html, env.task.wait)
		if #entries < (TradeValues.minimum[category] or 1) then return nil end
		for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do table.insert(values, entry) end
		env.task.wait(0.3)
	end
	return #values >= 650 and values or nil
end

function TradeValues.accept(state, timestamp, items, source)
	local mapping = TradeValues.build(items, TradeValues.database(state), state.context.environment.task.wait)
	if not mapping or mapping.n <= 300 then return false end
	state.mapped, state.status = mapping, "ok"
	state.t, state.n, state.src = tonumber(timestamp) or os.time(), mapping.n, source
	return true
end

function TradeValues.load(state, force)
	if state.busy then return end
	if not force and state.status == "ok" and os.time() - state.t < 7200 then return end
	if not force and os.clock() < state.nextTry then return end
	state.busy = true
	state.context.environment.task.spawn(function()
		local loaded = false
		pcall(function()
			local cached = TradeValues.readCache(state)
			if cached and not force and os.time() - cached.t < 7200 and state.status ~= "ok" then
				loaded = TradeValues.accept(state, cached.t, cached.items, "cache")
				if loaded then return end
			end
			local fresh = TradeValues.fetch(state)
			if fresh then
				local timestamp = os.time()
				pcall(function() state.context.environment.writefile("PulseHub/sv_values.json", state.context.http:JSONEncode({t = timestamp, items = fresh})) end)
				loaded = TradeValues.accept(state, timestamp, fresh, "site")
				if loaded then return end
			end
			if cached and (state.status ~= "ok" or cached.t > state.t) then loaded = TradeValues.accept(state, cached.t, cached.items, "old") end
		end)
		if loaded then state.tries, state.nextTry = 0, 0
		else
			if not state.mapped then state.status = "fail" end
			state.tries = math.min(state.tries + 1, 6)
			state.nextTry = os.clock() + 15 * 2 ^ state.tries
		end
		state.busy = false
	end)
end

function TradeValues.item(tile)
	local name = tile:FindFirstChild("ItemName")
	local label = name and name:FindFirstChild("Label")
	local text = label and label.Text or ""
	if text == "" or text:find("^Loading") then return nil, 1 end
	local container = tile:FindFirstChild("Container")
	local amount = container and container:FindFirstChild("Amount")
	local quantity = amount and amount:IsA("TextLabel") and tonumber(tostring(amount.Text):match("%d+")) or 1
	return text, quantity or 1
end

function TradeValues.label(state, parent, name, group)
	local env = state.context.environment
	local label = parent:FindFirstChild(name)
	if label and (not label:IsA("TextLabel") or label:GetAttribute("st") ~= "troll") then label:Destroy(); label = nil end
	if not label then
		label = env.Instance.new("TextLabel")
		label.Name, label.BackgroundColor3, label.BackgroundTransparency = name, env.Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 14), 0.15
		label.TextColor3, label.TextStrokeTransparency = env.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.45
		label.TextXAlignment, label.Font, label.TextSize = env.Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, env.Enum.Font.GothamBold, 14
		label.BorderSizePixel, label.ZIndex = 0, 61
		label:SetAttribute("st", "troll")
		label.Parent = parent
	end
	state.labels[label] = group
	return label
end

function TradeValues.scale(object)
	local current, scale = object, 1
	while current and not current:IsA("LayerCollector") do
		local ok, item = pcall(current.FindFirstChildOfClass, current, "UIScale")
		if ok and item then scale *= item.Scale end
		current = current.Parent
	end
	return math.max(scale, 0.05)
end

function TradeValues.badge(state, tile, value, force, group)
	local env = state.context.environment
	local container = tile:FindFirstChild("Container") or tile
	local label = container:FindFirstChild("__PulseIV")
	if value == nil and not force then if label then label.Visible = false end; return end
	label = TradeValues.label(state, container, "__PulseIV", group)
	local size = math.clamp(math.floor(container.AbsoluteSize.Y / TradeValues.scale(container) * 0.19 + 0.5), 14, 24)
	local text = value ~= nil and TradeValues.format(value) or "?"
	label.Position, label.Size, label.TextSize = env.UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3), env.UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(size * 2, #text * size * 0.65 + 14), size), math.max(10, math.floor(size * 0.86 + 0.5))
	label.Text, label.Visible = " " .. text, true
	label.TextColor3 = value ~= nil and env.Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or env.Color3.fromRGB(184, 186, 196)
end

function TradeValues.offer(state, offer, group)
	local container = offer and offer:FindFirstChild("Container")
	if not container then return 0, 0, 0 end
	local total, known, count = 0, 0, 0
	for _, tile in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
		if not tile:IsA("GuiObject") then continue end
		local name, quantity
		if tile.Visible then name, quantity = TradeValues.item(tile) end
		if not name then TradeValues.badge(state, tile, nil, false, group); continue end
		local value = TradeValues.valueOf(state, tile, name)
		count += quantity
		if value ~= nil then total += value * quantity; known += quantity end
		TradeValues.badge(state, tile, value, true, group)
	end
	return total, known, count
end

function TradeValues.summary(state, offer, total, known, count, verdict)
	if not offer then return end
	local label = offer:FindFirstChild("__PulseSum")
	if count == 0 then
		if label then label.Visible = false end
		local verdictLabel = offer:FindFirstChild("__PulseVerdict")
		if verdictLabel then verdictLabel.Visible = false end
		return
	end
	local env = state.context.environment
	label = TradeValues.label(state, offer, "__PulseSum", "trade")
	label.Position, label.Size, label.TextSize = env.UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 5), env.UDim2.fromOffset(115, 24), 14
	label.Text, label.Visible = " " .. TradeValues.format(total) .. (known < count and " +?" or ""), true
	local result = offer:FindFirstChild("__PulseVerdict")
	if not verdict then if result then result.Visible = false end; return end
	result = TradeValues.label(state, offer, "__PulseVerdict", "trade")
	result.Position, result.Size, result.TextSize = env.UDim2.new(0.5, 120, 0, 5), env.UDim2.fromOffset(140, 24), 14
	result.Text, result.Visible = " " .. verdict, true
end

function TradeValues.tiles(state, root, group)
	local total = 0
	if not root then return total end
	state.tileCache = state.tileCache or setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
	local now, cached = os.clock(), state.tileCache[root]
	local interval = group == "tradeInventory" and 1.2 or 2
	if not cached or now - cached.at >= interval then
		cached = {at = now, tiles = {}}
		for _, child in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
			if child.Name ~= "ItemName" or not child:IsA("GuiObject") then continue end
			local tile = child.Parent
			if tile and tile:FindFirstChild("Container") then table.insert(cached.tiles, tile) end
		end
		state.tileCache[root] = cached
	end
	for _, tile in ipairs(cached.tiles) do
		if not tile.Parent or tile.Visible == false then continue end
		local name, quantity = TradeValues.item(tile)
		local value = name and TradeValues.valueOf(state, tile, name) or nil
		if value ~= nil then total += value * quantity end
		TradeValues.badge(state, tile, value, false, group)
	end
	return total
end

function TradeValues.visible(object)
	local current = object
	while current and current:IsA("GuiObject") do
		if not current.Visible then return false end
		current = current.Parent
	end
	if current and current:IsA("ScreenGui") then return current.Enabled end
	return current ~= nil
end

function TradeValues.clear(state, group)
	for label, owner in pairs(state.labels) do
		if group and owner ~= group then continue end
		pcall(function() label:Destroy() end)
		state.labels[label] = nil
	end
end

function TradeValues.playerGui(state)
	return state.context.player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
end

function TradeValues.tradeGui(state)
	local gui = TradeValues.playerGui(state)
	if not gui then return nil end
	for _, name in ipairs({"TradeGUI", "TradeGUI_Phone"}) do
		local screen = gui:FindFirstChild(name)
		local container = screen and screen.Enabled and screen:FindFirstChild("Container")
		local trade = container and container:FindFirstChild("Trade")
		if trade and trade.Visible ~= false then return screen, container, trade end
	end
	return nil
end

function TradeValues.inventoryGui(state)
	local gui = TradeValues.playerGui(state)
	if not gui then return nil end
	for _, name in ipairs({"MainGUI", "MainMobile"}) do
		local screen = gui:FindFirstChild(name)
		local game = screen and screen.Enabled and screen:FindFirstChild("Game")
		local inventory = game and game:FindFirstChild("Inventory")
		if inventory and TradeValues.visible(inventory) then return inventory, name == "MainMobile" end
	end
	return nil
end

function TradeValues.tradePanel(state, container, give, get, knownGive, countGive, knownGet, countGet, inventory)
	local env = state.context.environment
	local panel = container:FindFirstChild("__PulsePanel")
	if panel and (not panel:IsA("Frame") or panel:GetAttribute("v") ~= 4) then panel:Destroy(); panel = nil end
	if not panel then
		panel = env.Instance.new("Frame")
		panel.Name, panel.BackgroundTransparency, panel.BorderSizePixel, panel.ZIndex = "__PulsePanel", 1, 0, 40
		panel.Position, panel.Size = env.UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 8), env.UDim2.new(0.5, -24, 0.25, -14)
		panel:SetAttribute("v", 4)
		panel.Parent = container
		for _, entry in ipairs({{"GiveL", "Left"}, {"GiveV", "Left"}, {"GiveS", "Left"},
			{"GetL", "Right"}, {"GetV", "Right"}, {"GetS", "Right"}, {"Src", "Right"}, {"Inv", "Left"}, {"Verdict", "Center"}}) do
			local label = env.Instance.new("TextLabel")
			label.Name, label.BackgroundTransparency, label.BorderSizePixel, label.ZIndex = entry[1], 1, 0, 42
			label.TextColor3, label.TextXAlignment, label.Font = env.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), env.Enum.TextXAlignment[entry[2]], env.Enum.Font.GothamBold
			label.Parent = panel
		end
		for _, name in ipairs({"GiveLine", "GetLine"}) do
			local line = env.Instance.new("Frame")
			line.Name, line.BackgroundColor3, line.BorderSizePixel, line.ZIndex = name, env.Color3.fromRGB(226, 46, 58), 0, 42
			if name == "GetLine" then line.AnchorPoint = env.Vector2.new(1, 0) end
			line.Parent = panel
		end
		local face = env.Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		face.Name, face.AnchorPoint, face.BackgroundTransparency, face.ZIndex = "Face", env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), 1, 42
		face.Image, face.ScaleType, face.Parent = "rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=9631028060&w=150&h=150", env.Enum.ScaleType.Fit, panel
	end
	state.labels[panel] = "trade"
	local uiScale = TradeValues.scale(panel)
	local width, height = panel.AbsoluteSize.X / uiScale, panel.AbsoluteSize.Y / uiScale
	if width < 40 or height < 30 then return end
	local language = state.context.configuration.ui and state.context.configuration.ui.lang or "en"
	local texts = ({ru = {"РћРўР”РђРЃРЁР¬", "РџРћР›РЈР§РђР•РЁР¬", "Р’Р«Р“РћР”РќРћ", "РќР•Р’Р«Р“РћР”РќРћ", "РџРћР РћР’РќРЈ", "РїСЂРµРґРј.", "Р±РµР· С†РµРЅС‹", "РёРЅРІРµРЅС‚Р°СЂСЊ", "РјРёРЅ", "С‡"},
		uk = {"Р’Р†Р”Р”РђР„РЁ", "РћРўР РРњРЈР„РЁ", "Р’РР“Р†Р”РќРћ", "РќР•Р’РР“Р†Р”РќРћ", "РџРћР Р†Р’РќРЈ", "РїСЂРµРґРј.", "Р±РµР· С†С–РЅРё", "С–РЅРІРµРЅС‚Р°СЂ", "С…РІ", "РіРѕРґ"}})[language]
		or {"YOU GIVE", "YOU GET", "WIN", "LOSS", "FAIR", "items", "no value", "inventory", "min", "h"}
	local kind, difference = TradeValues.verdict(give, get)
	local result = ({win = texts[3], loss = texts[4], even = texts[5]})[kind]
	local function countLabel(count, known)
		return tostring(count) .. " " .. texts[6] .. (count > known and " В· " .. (count - known) .. " " .. texts[7] or "")
	end
	local scale, sideWidth = height / 120, width * 0.36
	local function place(name, x, y, w, h, size, text)
		local label = panel:FindFirstChild(name)
		label.Position, label.Size, label.TextSize, label.Text = env.UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(x), math.floor(y)),
			env.UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(w), math.floor(h)), math.max(8, math.floor(size)), text
	end
	place("GiveL", 0, 4 * scale, sideWidth, 15 * scale, 14 * scale, texts[1])
	place("GiveV", 0, 18 * scale, sideWidth, 42 * scale, 44 * scale, TradeValues.format(give))
	place("GiveS", 0, 68 * scale, sideWidth, 14 * scale, 13 * scale, countLabel(countGive, knownGive))
	place("GetL", width - sideWidth, 4 * scale, sideWidth, 15 * scale, 14 * scale, texts[2])
	place("GetV", width - sideWidth, 18 * scale, sideWidth, 42 * scale, 44 * scale, TradeValues.format(get))
	place("GetS", width - sideWidth, 68 * scale, sideWidth, 14 * scale, 13 * scale, countLabel(countGet, knownGet))
	local lineWidth = math.floor(math.min(72, sideWidth * 0.5))
	local giveLine, getLine = panel:FindFirstChild("GiveLine"), panel:FindFirstChild("GetLine")
	giveLine.Position, giveLine.Size = env.UDim2.fromOffset(0, math.floor(62 * scale)), env.UDim2.fromOffset(lineWidth, 3)
	getLine.Position, getLine.Size = env.UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(width), math.floor(62 * scale)), env.UDim2.fromOffset(lineWidth, 3)
	local face = panel:FindFirstChild("Face")
	local faceSize = math.floor(math.min(58 * scale, width * 0.16))
	face.Size, face.Position = env.UDim2.fromOffset(faceSize, faceSize), env.UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(width / 2), math.floor(38 * scale))
	face.ImageTransparency, face.Rotation = countGive + countGet == 0 and 0.55 or (kind == "loss" and 0.45 or 0), kind == "win" and -8 or 0
	local verdict = panel:FindFirstChild("Verdict")
	place("Verdict", width * 0.32, 84 * scale, width * 0.36, 24 * scale, 14 * scale,
		result .. (difference and "  " .. difference or ""))
	verdict.Visible = countGive + countGet > 0
	verdict.TextColor3 = kind == "win" and env.Color3.fromRGB(40, 196, 104) or kind == "loss" and env.Color3.fromRGB(226, 46, 58) or env.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local age = math.max(0, os.time() - state.t)
	local source = state.t > 0 and "supremevalues В· " .. (age < 3600 and math.floor(age / 60) .. " " .. texts[9] or math.floor(age / 3600) .. " " .. texts[10]) or ""
	place("Src", width - sideWidth, 108 * scale, sideWidth, 12 * scale, 11 * scale, source)
	place("Inv", 0, 108 * scale, sideWidth, 12 * scale, 11 * scale, inventory > 0 and texts[8] .. " " .. TradeValues.format(inventory) or "")
	panel.Visible = true
end

function TradeValues.scanTrade(state)
	local screen, container, trade = TradeValues.tradeGui(state)
	if not screen or not state.settings.value then
		TradeValues.clear(state, "trade")
		TradeValues.clear(state, "tradeInventory")
		return
	end
	TradeValues.load(state, false)
	if state.status ~= "ok" then return end
	local yours, theirs = trade:FindFirstChild("YourOffer"), trade:FindFirstChild("TheirOffer")
	local give, knownGive, countGive = TradeValues.offer(state, yours, "trade")
	local get, knownGet, countGet = TradeValues.offer(state, theirs, "trade")
	local kind, difference = TradeValues.verdict(give, get)
	local language = state.context.configuration.ui and state.context.configuration.ui.lang or "en"
	local labels = ({ru = {win = "Р’Р«Р“РћР”РќРћ", loss = "РќР•Р’Р«Р“РћР”РќРћ", even = "РџРћР РћР’РќРЈ"},
		uk = {win = "Р’РР“Р†Р”РќРћ", loss = "РќР•Р’РР“Р†Р”РќРћ", even = "РџРћР Р†Р’РќРЈ"}})[language] or {win = "WIN", loss = "LOSS", even = "FAIR"}
	local verdict = labels[kind] .. (difference and "  " .. difference or "")
	TradeValues.summary(state, yours, give, knownGive, countGive)
	TradeValues.summary(state, theirs, get, knownGet, countGet, screen.Name == "TradeGUI_Phone" and verdict or nil)
	local inventory = 0
	if state.settings.inv ~= false then
		local items = container:FindFirstChild("Items")
		local main = items and items:FindFirstChild("Main")
		if main and TradeValues.visible(main) then inventory = TradeValues.tiles(state, main, "tradeInventory") end
	else TradeValues.clear(state, "tradeInventory") end
	if screen.Name ~= "TradeGUI_Phone" then TradeValues.tradePanel(state, container, give, get, knownGive, countGive, knownGet, countGet, inventory) end
end

function TradeValues.scanInventory(state)
	local inventory, mobile = TradeValues.inventoryGui(state)
	if not inventory or state.settings.inv == false then TradeValues.clear(state, "inventory"); return end
	TradeValues.load(state, false)
	if state.status ~= "ok" then return end
	local main = inventory:FindFirstChild("Main")
	local weapons, pets = main and main:FindFirstChild("Weapons"), main and main:FindFirstChild("Pets")
	local weaponTotal = TradeValues.tiles(state, weapons and weapons:FindFirstChild("Items"), "inventory")
	local petTotal = TradeValues.tiles(state, pets and pets:FindFirstChild("Items"), "inventory")
	local total = weaponTotal + petTotal
	local badge = inventory:FindFirstChild("__PulseInvBadge")
	if mobile or total <= 0 then if badge then badge.Visible = false end; return end
	local env = state.context.environment
	badge = TradeValues.label(state, inventory, "__PulseInvBadge", "inventory")
	badge.AnchorPoint, badge.Position, badge.Size = env.Vector2.new(0, 1), env.UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, -6), env.UDim2.fromOffset(360, 44)
	local language = state.context.configuration.ui and state.context.configuration.ui.lang or "en"
	local labels = ({ru = {"РРќР’Р•РќРўРђР Р¬", "РѕСЂСѓР¶РёРµ", "РїРёС‚РѕРјС†С‹"}, uk = {"Р†РќР’Р•РќРўРђР ", "Р·Р±СЂРѕСЏ", "СѓР»СЋР±Р»РµРЅС†С–"}})[language]
		or {"INVENTORY", "weapons", "pets"}
	badge.Text, badge.TextSize, badge.Visible = " " .. labels[1] .. "  " .. TradeValues.format(total) .. "   В·   " .. labels[2] .. " "
		.. TradeValues.format(weaponTotal) .. "   В·   " .. labels[3] .. " " .. TradeValues.format(petTotal), 15, true
end

function TradeValues.statusText(state)
	local language = state.context.configuration.ui and state.context.configuration.ui.lang or "en"
	local texts = ({ru = {"Р¦РµРЅС‹", "Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°", "РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРЅС‹", "РјРёРЅ", "С‡", "РЅР°Р·Р°Рґ", "РїСЂРµРґРјРµС‚РѕРІ"},
		uk = {"Р¦С–РЅРё", "Р·Р°РІР°РЅС‚Р°Р¶РµРЅРЅСЏ", "РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРЅС–", "С…РІ", "РіРѕРґ", "С‚РѕРјСѓ", "РїСЂРµРґРјРµС‚С–РІ"}})[language]
		or {"Values", "loading", "unavailable", "min", "h", "ago", "items"}
	if state.status ~= "ok" then return texts[1] .. ": " .. (state.status == "fail" and not state.busy and texts[3] or texts[2]) end
	local age = math.max(0, os.time() - state.t)
	local elapsed = age < 3600 and math.floor(age / 60) .. " " .. texts[4] or math.floor(age / 3600) .. " " .. texts[5]
	return string.format("%s: %d %s В· %s %s В· supremevalues.com", texts[1], state.n, texts[7], elapsed, texts[6])
end

function TradeValues.install(context)
	local configuration, env, section = context.configuration, context.environment, context.section
	configuration.trade = configuration.trade or {}
	local settings = configuration.trade
	if settings.value == nil then settings.value = false end
	local state = {context = context, settings = settings, status = "idle", t = 0, n = 0, busy = false, tries = 0, nextTry = 0, src = "", labels = {}}
	state.valueOf = function(tile, name) return TradeValues.valueOf(state, tile, name) end
	state.byId = function(id)
		local entry = state.mapped and state.mapped.byKey["i:" .. tostring(id)]
		return entry and entry.v or nil
	end
	state.load = function(force) TradeValues.load(state, force) end
	env.getgenv().__pulseSV = state
	section:Header("Trade")
	section:Toggle({Text = "Trade Value Counter", Default = settings.value == true, Callback = function(enabled)
		settings.value = enabled == true
		if enabled then TradeValues.load(state, false) else TradeValues.clear(state, "trade") end
	end})
	section:Toggle({Text = "Inventory Values", Default = settings.inv ~= false, Callback = function(enabled)
		settings.inv = enabled == true
		if enabled then TradeValues.load(state, false)
		else TradeValues.clear(state, "inventory"); TradeValues.clear(state, "tradeInventory") end
	end})
	local note = section:Note({Text = "supremevalues.com"})
	local tradeTime, inventoryTime, noteTime, refreshTime = 0, 0, 0, 0
	if context.runService.Heartbeat then context.connect(context.runService.Heartbeat, function(delta)
		tradeTime += delta
		inventoryTime += delta
		noteTime += delta
		refreshTime += delta
		if tradeTime >= 0.3 then tradeTime = 0; pcall(TradeValues.scanTrade, state) end
		if inventoryTime >= 0.6 then inventoryTime = 0; pcall(TradeValues.scanInventory, state) end
		if noteTime >= 2 then
			noteTime = 0
			pcall(function() if note and note.Parent then note.Text = TradeValues.statusText(state) end end)
		end
		if refreshTime >= 300 then
			refreshTime = 0
			if settings.value or settings.inv ~= false then TradeValues.load(state, false) end
		end
	end) end
	if settings.value or settings.inv ~= false then env.task.delay(3, function() if context.isAlive() then TradeValues.load(state, false) end end) end
	return state, function()
		TradeValues.clear(state)
		if env.getgenv().__pulseSV == state then env.getgenv().__pulseSV = nil end
	end
end

function Boombox.initialize(configuration)
	if type(configuration.music) ~= "table" then configuration.music = {} end
	local settings = configuration.music
	if settings.auto == nil then settings.auto = false end
	if settings.random == nil then settings.random = false end
	settings.delay, settings.volume = tonumber(settings.delay) or 3, tonumber(settings.volume) or 50
	if type(settings.custom) ~= "table" then settings.custom = {} end
	if type(settings.hidden) ~= "table" then settings.hidden = {} end
	if type(settings.info) ~= "table" then settings.info = {} end
	settings.song = tostring(settings.song or "")
	if settings.song == "" then settings.song = BOOMBOX_TRACKS[1].id end
	return settings
end

function Boombox.tracks(state)
	local result = {}
	for _, track in ipairs(BOOMBOX_TRACKS) do
		if not state.settings.hidden[track.id] then table.insert(result, track) end
	end
	for index, track in ipairs(state.settings.custom) do
		if type(track) ~= "table" then continue end
		table.insert(result, {id = tostring(track.id), name = track.name, lang = track.lang, customIndex = index})
	end
	return result
end

function Boombox.track(state, id)
	for _, track in ipairs(Boombox.tracks(state)) do if track.id == tostring(id) then return track end end
	return nil
end

function Boombox.displayName(state, track)
	local name = tostring(track.name or "")
	local info = state.settings.info[tostring(track.id)]
	if name == "" or name == tostring(track.id) or name:match("^Track %d+$") then
		if type(info) == "table" and info.n then return info.n end
	end
	return name ~= "" and name or tostring(track.id)
end

function Boombox.radio(state)
	local character = state.context.workspace:FindFirstChild(state.context.player.Name)
	local radio = character and character:FindFirstChild("Radio")
	return radio and radio:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sound")
end

function Boombox.remote(state)
	if state.playRemote and state.playRemote.Parent then return state.playRemote end
	local remotes = state.context.storage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
	local inventory = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Inventory")
	state.playRemote = inventory and inventory:FindFirstChild("PlaySong")
	return state.playRemote
end

function Boombox.volume(state)
	local sound = Boombox.radio(state)
	if sound then sound.Volume = state.settings.volume / 100 end
end

function Boombox.play(state, id)
	local remote = Boombox.remote(state)
	if not remote or not id or tostring(id) == "" then return false end
	local asset = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)
	local sound = Boombox.radio(state)
	if sound and sound.IsPlaying and sound.SoundId == asset then return true end
	pcall(function() remote:FireServer(asset) end)
	state.context.environment.task.delay(0.6, function() if state.context.isAlive() then pcall(Boombox.volume, state) end end)
	return true
end

function Boombox.stop(state)
	local sound, remote = Boombox.radio(state), Boombox.remote(state)
	if not sound or not remote or not sound.IsPlaying or sound.SoundId == "" then return end
	pcall(function() remote:FireServer(sound.SoundId) end)
end

function Boombox.choose(state)
	local tracks = Boombox.tracks(state)
	if state.settings.random and #tracks > 0 then return tracks[math.random(1, #tracks)].id end
	return state.settings.song
end

function Boombox.select(state, id)
	state.settings.song = tostring(id)
	if state.context.player:GetAttribute("Radio") then Boombox.play(state, id)
	else pcall(function() state.context.window:Notify({Title = state.context.translation.tr("Radio not equipped"), Kind = "warning", Duration = 3}) end) end
	if state.update then state.update() end
end

function Boombox.move(state, direction)
	local tracks = Boombox.tracks(state)
	if #tracks == 0 then return end
	local current = 0
	for index, track in ipairs(tracks) do if track.id == state.settings.song then current = index; break end end
	local selected
	if state.settings.random and #tracks > 1 then
		repeat selected = math.random(1, #tracks) until selected ~= current
	else selected = current == 0 and (direction > 0 and 1 or #tracks) or (current - 1 + direction) % #tracks + 1 end
	Boombox.select(state, tracks[selected].id)
end

function Boombox.add(state, source, name)
	source = tostring(source or "")
	local id = source:match("(%d%d%d%d%d+)")
	if not id then return false end
	if state.settings.hidden[id] then
		state.settings.hidden[id], state.settings.song = nil, id
		return true
	end
	if Boombox.track(state, id) then state.settings.song = id; return true end
	name = tostring(name or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
	if name == "" then name = (source:match("/%d%d%d%d%d+/([^/%?#]+)") or ""):gsub("[%-_]+", " ") end
	table.insert(state.settings.custom, {id = id, name = name ~= "" and name or id})
	return true
end

function Boombox.remove(state, id)
	id = tostring(id)
	local track = Boombox.track(state, id)
	if not track then return end
	if track.customIndex then table.remove(state.settings.custom, track.customIndex) else state.settings.hidden[id] = true end
	if state.settings.song == id then
		local tracks = Boombox.tracks(state)
		state.settings.song = tracks[1] and tracks[1].id or ""
	end
end

function Boombox.roundStart(state)
	local context, settings = state.context, state.settings
	if not settings.auto or not context.player:GetAttribute("Radio") then return end
	state.generation += 1
	local generation = state.generation
	context.environment.task.delay(math.max(0, settings.delay), function()
		if generation ~= state.generation or not context.isAlive() or not settings.auto or not context.player:GetAttribute("Radio") then return end
		local started = os.clock()
		while context.isAlive() and generation == state.generation and os.clock() - started < 10 do
			local character = context.player.Character
			local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and Boombox.radio(state) then break end
			context.environment.task.wait(0.25)
		end
		if generation ~= state.generation or not context.isAlive() or not settings.auto then return end
		local id = Boombox.choose(state)
		for _ = 1, 3 do
			Boombox.play(state, id)
			context.environment.task.wait(2)
			if generation ~= state.generation or not context.isAlive() or not settings.auto then return end
			local sound = Boombox.radio(state)
			if sound and sound.IsPlaying then return end
		end
	end)
end

function Boombox.fetchInfo(state, id)
	id = tostring(id)
	if state.settings.info[id] or state.pending[id] then return end
	state.pending[id] = true
	table.insert(state.infoQueue, id)
	if state.fetching then return end
	state.fetching = true
	state.context.environment.task.spawn(function()
		while state.context.isAlive() and #state.infoQueue > 0 do
			local nextId = table.remove(state.infoQueue, 1)
			local ok, info = pcall(function()
				return state.context.marketplace:GetProductInfo(tonumber(nextId), state.context.environment.Enum.InfoType.Asset)
			end)
			if ok and type(info) == "table" then
				state.settings.info[nextId] = {n = type(info.Name) == "string" and info.Name ~= "" and info.Name or nil,
					c = type(info.Creator) == "table" and info.Creator.Name or nil}
				if state.update then state.update() end
			end
			state.context.environment.task.wait(0.15)
		end
		state.fetching = false
	end)
end

function Boombox.status(state)
	local sound = Boombox.radio(state)
	local playing = sound and sound.IsPlaying and sound.SoundId ~= "" or false
	local id = playing and (sound.SoundId:match("id=(%d+)") or sound.SoundId:match("(%d+)")) or nil
	return not not state.context.player:GetAttribute("Radio"), playing, id
end

function Boombox.playerUI(state)
	local context, env = state.context, state.context.environment
	local container = context.section:Container()
	if not container then return end
	local create = context.create
	local frame = create("Frame", {Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 72), BackgroundTransparency = 1}, container)
	local picture = create("Frame", {Position = env.UDim2.fromOffset(2, 4), Size = env.UDim2.fromOffset(64, 64), BorderSizePixel = 0}, frame)
	context.corner(picture, 14)
	context.nova.Theme.bind(picture, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
	context.nova.icon({Parent = picture, Icon = "music", Size = 26, Pos = env.UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Anchor = env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Color = "OnAccent", Z = 3})
	local title = context.nova.label({Parent = frame, Text = "", Font = context.nova.Font.Bold(), Size = 15,
		Pos = env.UDim2.fromOffset(80, 14), Box = env.UDim2.new(1, -262, 0, 20), Truncate = true, Color = "Text"})
	local status = context.nova.label({Parent = frame, Text = "", Font = context.nova.Font.Medium(), Size = 12,
		Pos = env.UDim2.fromOffset(80, 38), Box = env.UDim2.new(1, -262, 0, 16), Truncate = true, Color = "Muted"})
	local controls = create("Frame", {AnchorPoint = env.Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = env.UDim2.new(1, -4, 0.5, 0),
		Size = env.UDim2.fromOffset(0, 46), AutomaticSize = env.Enum.AutomaticSize.X, BackgroundTransparency = 1}, frame)
	create("UIListLayout", {FillDirection = env.Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, HorizontalAlignment = env.Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right,
		VerticalAlignment = env.Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, Padding = env.UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = env.Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, controls)
	local function button(icon, size, order, primary, callback)
		local item = create("TextButton", {Size = env.UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0,
			LayoutOrder = order, BackgroundTransparency = primary and 0 or 1}, controls)
		context.corner(item, math.floor(size / 2))
		context.nova.Theme.bind(item, "BackgroundColor3", primary and "Accent" or "Surface2")
		local image = context.nova.icon({Parent = item, Icon = icon, Size = primary and 18 or 16, Pos = env.UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
			Anchor = env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Color = primary and "OnAccent" or "Muted", Z = 3})
		context.connect(item.MouseButton1Click, callback)
		return image
	end
	local shuffle = button("shuffle", 32, 1, false, function()
		if state.randomControl then state.randomControl.Set(not state.settings.random) end
		if state.update then state.update() end
	end)
	button("skip-back", 34, 2, false, function() Boombox.move(state, -1) end)
	local play = button("play", 46, 3, true, function()
		local _, playing = Boombox.status(state)
		if playing then Boombox.stop(state) else Boombox.select(state, Boombox.choose(state)) end
	end)
	button("skip-forward", 34, 4, false, function() Boombox.move(state, 1) end)
	state.paintPlayer = function()
		local equipped, playing, playingId = Boombox.status(state)
		local id = playing and playingId or state.settings.song
		local track = Boombox.track(state, id) or (id and {id = id, name = id})
		title.Text = track and Boombox.displayName(state, track) or context.translation.tr("Nothing selected")
		local text = not equipped and "Radio not equipped" or playing and "Playing" or "Stopped"
		status.Text = context.translation.tr(text) .. (track and ("  В·  " .. (track.lang or "ID " .. track.id)) or "")
		pcall(context.nova.Icons.apply, play, playing and "square" or "play")
		context.nova.paint(shuffle, "ImageColor3", state.settings.random and "Accent" or "Muted", "Quick")
	end
end

function Boombox.listUI(state)
	local context, env = state.context, state.context.environment
	local section, create = context.section, context.create
	local inputContainer = section:Container()
	local frame = create("Frame", {Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), BackgroundTransparency = 1}, inputContainer)
	local function input(icon, placeholder, position, size)
		local base = create("Frame", {Position = position, Size = size, BorderSizePixel = 0}, frame)
		context.corner(base, 9)
		context.nova.Theme.bind(base, "BackgroundColor3", "Surface2")
		context.nova.icon({Parent = base, Icon = icon, Size = 14, Pos = env.UDim2.new(0, 11, 0.5, 0), Anchor = env.Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Color = "Faint", Z = 2})
		local field = create("TextBox", {Position = env.UDim2.fromOffset(32, 0), Size = env.UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			BorderSizePixel = 0, ClearTextOnFocus = false, Text = "", PlaceholderText = context.translation.tr(placeholder),
			TextSize = 13, TextXAlignment = env.Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = env.Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, ZIndex = 2}, base)
		field.FontFace = context.nova.Font.Medium()
		context.nova.Theme.bind(field, "TextColor3", "Text")
		context.nova.Theme.bind(field, "PlaceholderColor3", "Faint")
		context.translation.bind(field, placeholder, "PlaceholderText")
		return field
	end
	local id = input("link", "Sound ID or link", env.UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), env.UDim2.new(0.56, -8, 1, 0))
	local name = input("type", "Name (optional)", env.UDim2.new(0.56, 0, 0, 0), env.UDim2.new(0.44, -46, 1, 0))
	local add = create("TextButton", {AnchorPoint = env.Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = env.UDim2.fromScale(1, 0), Size = env.UDim2.fromOffset(38, 36),
		Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0}, frame)
	context.corner(add, 9)
	context.nova.Theme.bind(add, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
	context.nova.icon({Parent = add, Icon = "plus", Size = 18, Pos = env.UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Anchor = env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Color = "OnAccent", Z = 2})
	local function addTrack()
		if not Boombox.add(state, id.Text, name.Text) then return end
		id.Text, name.Text = "", ""
		state.refreshList()
	end
	context.connect(add.MouseButton1Click, addTrack)
	context.connect(id.FocusLost, function(enter) if enter then addTrack() end end)
	context.connect(name.FocusLost, function(enter) if enter then addTrack() end end)
	local list = section:Container()
	local rows = {}
	state.paintList = function()
		local _, playing, playingId = Boombox.status(state)
		for _, item in ipairs(rows) do
			local active = playing and playingId == item.track.id
			local selected = state.settings.song == item.track.id
			context.nova.paint(item.row, "BackgroundColor3", active and "Accent" or "Surface2", "Quick")
			item.row.BackgroundTransparency = active and 0.88 or selected and 0.45 or 1
			pcall(context.nova.Icons.apply, item.icon, active and "square" or "music")
			item.label.Text = Boombox.displayName(state, item.track)
		end
	end
	state.refreshList = function()
		for _, item in ipairs(rows) do pcall(function() item.row:Destroy() end) end
		table.clear(rows)
		local tracks = Boombox.tracks(state)
		for index, track in ipairs(tracks) do
			local row = create("TextButton", {Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 50), Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
				BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundTransparency = 0.65, LayoutOrder = index}, list)
			context.corner(row, 11)
			context.nova.Theme.bind(row, "BackgroundColor3", "Surface2")
			local icon = context.nova.icon({Parent = row, Icon = "music", Size = 17, Pos = env.UDim2.new(0, 17, 0.5, 0),
				Anchor = env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Color = "Muted", Z = 2})
			local label = context.nova.label({Parent = row, Text = Boombox.displayName(state, track), Font = context.nova.Font.Semi(), Size = 13,
				Pos = env.UDim2.fromOffset(36, 8), Box = env.UDim2.new(1, -80, 0, 18), Truncate = true, Color = "Text"})
			context.nova.label({Parent = row, Text = track.lang or "ID " .. track.id, Font = context.nova.Font.Medium(), Size = 11,
				Pos = env.UDim2.fromOffset(36, 28), Box = env.UDim2.new(1, -80, 0, 14), Truncate = true, Color = "Faint"})
			local remove = create("TextButton", {AnchorPoint = env.Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = env.UDim2.new(1, -9, 0.5, 0),
				Size = env.UDim2.fromOffset(30, 30), Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundTransparency = 1}, row)
			context.nova.icon({Parent = remove, Icon = "trash-2", Size = 14, Pos = env.UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
				Anchor = env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Color = "Muted", Z = 2})
			local armed = false
			row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local _, playing, playingId = Boombox.status(state)
				if playing and playingId == track.id then Boombox.stop(state) else Boombox.select(state, track.id) end
			end)
			remove.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				if armed then Boombox.remove(state, track.id); state.refreshList(); return end
				armed = true
				context.nova.paint(remove, "BackgroundColor3", "Danger", "Quick")
				remove.BackgroundTransparency = 0.78
				env.task.delay(2, function() if remove.Parent then armed = false; remove.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end end)
			end)
			table.insert(rows, {row = row, icon = icon, label = label, track = track})
		end
		if state.update then state.update() end
		for _, track in ipairs(tracks) do Boombox.fetchInfo(state, track.id) end
	end
end

function Boombox.install(context)
	local env, section = context.environment, context.section
	local settings = Boombox.initialize(context.configuration)
	local state = {context = context, settings = settings, generation = 0, pending = {}, infoQueue = {}}
	state.update = function()
		if state.paintPlayer then pcall(state.paintPlayer) end
		if state.paintList then pcall(state.paintList) end
	end
	section:Sub("Music")
	section:Header("Music Player")
	pcall(Boombox.playerUI, state)
	section:Slider({Text = "Volume (local)", Min = 0, Max = 100, Default = 50, Decimals = 0, Suffix = "%", Callback = function(value)
		settings.volume = value
		pcall(Boombox.volume, state)
	end})
	section:Toggle({Text = "Auto Play on Round Start", Default = false, Callback = function(value) settings.auto = value end, Settings = function()
		state.randomControl = section:Toggle({Text = "Random song each round", Default = false, Callback = function(value)
			settings.random = value
			state.update()
		end})
		section:Slider({Text = "Delay after round start (sec)", Min = 0, Max = 15, Default = 3, Decimals = 0, Suffix = "s",
			Callback = function(value) settings.delay = value end})
	end})
	section:Header("My Songs")
	pcall(Boombox.listUI, state)
	section:Button({Text = "Restore", Callback = function()
		table.clear(settings.hidden)
		if state.refreshList then state.refreshList() end
	end})
	if state.refreshList then state.refreshList() end
	local gameplay
	pcall(function()
		local remotes = context.storage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
		gameplay = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Gameplay")
	end)
	local start = gameplay and gameplay:FindFirstChild("RoundStart")
	local finish = gameplay and gameplay:FindFirstChild("RoundEndFade")
	if start and start.OnClientEvent then context.connect(start.OnClientEvent, function() Boombox.roundStart(state) end) end
	if finish and finish.OnClientEvent then context.connect(finish.OnClientEvent, function() state.generation += 1 end) end
	local elapsed = 0
	if context.runService.Heartbeat then context.connect(context.runService.Heartbeat, function(delta)
		elapsed += delta
		if elapsed < 0.5 then return end
		elapsed = 0
		pcall(function()
			local sound = Boombox.radio(state)
			if sound and sound.IsPlaying and math.abs(sound.Volume - settings.volume / 100) > 0.01 then Boombox.volume(state) end
			state.update()
		end)
	end) end
	return state, function() state.generation += 1 end
end

local GunFeature = {}

function GunFeature.installEsp(context)
	local environment = context.environment
	local workspace, player = context.workspace, context.player
	local drops = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
	local watching, checkedAt, current, adornee = false, 0, nil, nil
	local billboard, highlight, onDrop

	local function remember(part)
		if part.Name == "GunDrop" and part:IsA("BasePart") then
			drops[part] = true
			if onDrop then environment.task.spawn(onDrop, part) end
		end
	end
	local function findDrop()
		if not watching then
			watching = true
			context.connect(workspace.DescendantAdded, remember)
			for _, part in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do remember(part) end
		end
		for part in pairs(drops) do
			if part.Parent and part.Name == "GunDrop" and part:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then return part end
			drops[part] = nil
		end
		return nil
	end
	local function refresh()
		local now = environment.os.clock()
		if now - checkedAt < 0.1 then return end
		checkedAt, current, adornee = now, nil, nil
		local drop = findDrop()
		if drop then
			current, adornee = drop, drop
			return
		end
		for _, object in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if object:IsA("Tool") and (object.Name == "Gun" or object:FindFirstChild("Shoot"))
				and not context.players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(object.Parent) then
				local part = object:FindFirstChild("Handle") or object:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
				if part then
					current, adornee = object, part
					return
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local function clear()
		if billboard then
			pcall(function() billboard:Destroy() end)
			billboard = nil
		end
		if highlight then
			pcall(function() highlight:Destroy() end)
			highlight = nil
		end
	end
	context.connect(context.runService.RenderStepped, function()
		if not context.configuration.sheriff.gunEsp then
			clear()
			return
		end
		refresh()
		if not current or not adornee or not adornee.Parent then
			clear()
			return
		end
		local color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 60)
		if not billboard or not billboard.Parent then
			billboard = environment.Instance.new("BillboardGui")
			billboard.Name, billboard.Size = "PulseGunESP", environment.UDim2.fromOffset(120, 28)
			billboard.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = environment.Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
			billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
			local label = environment.Instance.new("TextLabel")
			label.BackgroundTransparency, label.Size = 1, environment.UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			label.Font, label.TextSize, label.Text = environment.Enum.Font.GothamBold, 14, "GUN"
			label.TextColor3, label.TextStrokeTransparency, label.Parent = color, 0.4, billboard
			pcall(function() billboard.Parent = context.coreGui end)
			if billboard.Parent ~= context.coreGui then billboard.Parent = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") end
			pcall(function() environment.getgenv().__pulseBBMirror.add(billboard) end)
		end
		billboard.Adornee = adornee
		if not highlight or not highlight.Parent then
			highlight = environment.Instance.new("Highlight")
			highlight.Name, highlight.DepthMode = "PulseGunHL", environment.Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
			highlight.FillColor, highlight.OutlineColor = color, color
			highlight.FillTransparency, highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0.4, 0
			pcall(function() highlight.Parent = context.coreGui end)
			if highlight.Parent ~= context.coreGui then pcall(function() highlight.Parent = adornee end) end
		end
		if highlight.Adornee ~= adornee then highlight.Adornee = adornee end
	end)
	context.section:Toggle({Text = "Gun ESP", Default = context.defaults.sheriff.gunEsp,
		Callback = function(enabled) context.configuration.sheriff.gunEsp = enabled end})
	return clear, {
		findDrop = findDrop,
		find = function()
			local drop = findDrop()
			if drop then return drop, drop end
			refresh()
			if current and adornee and adornee.Parent then return current, adornee end
			return nil, nil
		end,
		onDrop = function(callback) onDrop = callback end,
	}
end

function GunFeature.installAuto(context)
	local environment = context.environment
	local globals, player = environment.getgenv(), context.player
	local settings = context.configuration.sheriff
	local state = {drop = nil, t0 = 0, n = 0, fullT = 0, log = {}}
	local picking = false
	globals.__pulseAutoGun = state

	local function log(message)
		table.insert(state.log, string.format("%.2f %s", environment.os.clock(), message))
		if #state.log > 30 then table.remove(state.log, 1) end
	end
	local function gun()
		local character = player.Character
		local held = character and character:FindFirstChild("Gun")
		if held then return held end
		local backpack = player:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
		return backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Gun")
	end
	local function root()
		local character = player.Character
		return character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	end
	local function allowed(force)
		if not force and not settings.autoGun or gun() then return false end
		local character = player.Character
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return false end
		local ok, roles = pcall(context.roles)
		local info = ok and type(roles) == "table" and roles[player.Name]
		if type(info) == "table" then
			if info.Dead == true or info.Killed == true then return false end
			local role = info.Role and tostring(info.Role):lower() or ""
			return role ~= "murderer" and role ~= "sheriff"
		end
		if ok and type(roles) == "table" and next(roles) ~= nil then return false end
		if character:FindFirstChild("Knife") then return false end
		local backpack = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
		return not (backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Knife"))
	end
	local function touch(object, part, full)
		local ownRoot = root()
		if not ownRoot or not part or not part.Parent or not environment.firetouchinterest then return false end
		local function fire(first, second)
			environment.firetouchinterest(first, second, 0)
			environment.firetouchinterest(first, second, 1)
		end
		pcall(fire, ownRoot, part)
		if full then
			local character = player.Character
			if character then
				for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("BasePart") and child ~= ownRoot then pcall(fire, child, part) end
				end
			end
			if object and object ~= part then
				for _, child in ipairs(object:GetDescendants()) do
					if child:IsA("BasePart") and child ~= part then pcall(fire, ownRoot, child) end
				end
			end
		end
		return true
	end
	local function pauseInvisibility(drop)
		local invisible = globals.__pulseInvis
		if not invisible or not invisible.active then return end
		local now = environment.os.clock()
		if globals.__pulseInvisPauseDrop ~= drop then
			globals.__pulseInvisPauseDrop, globals.__pulseInvisPauseN = drop, 0
		end
		if (tonumber(globals.__pulseInvisPause) or 0) < now and (globals.__pulseInvisPauseN or 0) < 4 then
			globals.__pulseInvisPauseN = (globals.__pulseInvisPauseN or 0) + 1
			globals.__pulseInvisPause = now + 0.6
		end
	end
	local function attempt(force)
		if not allowed(force) then return false end
		local object, part = context.drops.find()
		if not part then return false end
		local ownRoot = root()
		if not force and (tonumber(settings.agDist) or 1000) < 1000 and ownRoot
			and (ownRoot.Position - part.Position).Magnitude > (tonumber(settings.agDist) or 1000) then return false end
		local now = environment.os.clock()
		if state.drop ~= part then
			state.drop, state.t0, state.n, state.fullT, state.goneT = part, now, 0, 0, nil
			local distance = ownRoot and math.floor((ownRoot.Position - part.Position).Magnitude) or -1
			log("DROP d=" .. distance)
		end
		pauseInvisibility(object)
		local full = now - state.fullT > 0.12
		if full then state.fullT = now end
		if touch(object, part, full) then state.n += 1 end
		return true
	end
	globals.__pulsePickUpGun = function()
		if picking or gun() then return false end
		picking = true
		environment.task.spawn(function()
			local started, found = environment.os.clock(), false
			log("BIND")
			while context.isAlive() and environment.os.clock() - started < 2 and not gun() do
				local ok, success = pcall(attempt, true)
				if ok and success then
					found = true
				end
				if not found and environment.os.clock() - started > 0.25 then
					break
				end
				context.runService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			end
			picking = false
		end)
		return true
	end
	context.drops.onDrop(function(part)
		if not settings.autoGun then return end
		local started = environment.os.clock()
		while part.Parent and environment.os.clock() - started < 0.6 and not part:FindFirstChildOfClass("TouchTransmitter") do
			context.runService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		end
		if part.Parent then pcall(attempt) end
	end)
	environment.task.spawn(function()
		pcall(context.drops.findDrop)
		local hadGun = false
		while context.isAlive() do
			context.runService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			local hasGun, drop = gun() ~= nil, state.drop
			if drop then
				local now = environment.os.clock()
				if hasGun and not hadGun then
					log("GOT n=" .. state.n .. " dt=" .. string.format("%.2f", now - state.t0))
					state.drop = nil
				end
				if not (hasGun and not hadGun) and not drop.Parent then
					state.goneT = state.goneT or now
					if hasGun or now - state.goneT > 0.6 then
						log((hasGun and "GOT" or "GONE") .. " n=" .. state.n .. " dt=" .. string.format("%.2f", state.goneT - state.t0))
						state.drop, state.goneT = nil, nil
					end
				end
			end
			if settings.autoGun then pcall(attempt) end
			hadGun = hasGun
		end
	end)
	context.section:Toggle({Text = "Auto Pickup Gun", Default = context.defaults.sheriff.autoGun,
		Callback = function(enabled) settings.autoGun = enabled end, Settings = function()
			context.section:Slider({Text = "Pickup Distance", Min = 10, Max = 1000,
				Default = tonumber(settings.agDist) or 1000, Decimals = 0,
				Display = function(value)
					value = tonumber(value) or 0
					return value >= 1000 and "в€ћ" or tostring(math.floor(value))
				end,
				Callback = function(value) settings.agDist = value end})
		end})
	local key = globals.__pulseBindFromName(settings.grabKey)
	context.section:Keybind({Text = "Pick Up Gun", Default = key, Callback = function(value)
		key = value
		settings.grabKey = value and value.Name or ""
	end})
	context.connect(context.inputService.InputBegan, function(input)
		if not globals.__pulseBindReady() or not globals.__pulseBindHit(input, key) then return end
		if globals.__pulsePickUpGun then pcall(globals.__pulsePickUpGun) end
	end)
end

local TeleportFeature = {}

function TeleportFeature.install(context)
	local environment = context.environment
	local workspace = context.workspace
	local function move(target)
		local character = context.player.Character
		local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not root or not target then return false end
		pcall(function() root.CFrame = target end)
		return true
	end
	local function teleportPlayer(name)
		if not name then return false end
		local player = context.players:FindFirstChild(name)
		if not player or player == context.player then return false end
		local character = player.Character
		local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not root then return false end
		return move(root.CFrame * environment.CFrame.new(0, 0, 5))
	end
	local function findRole(role)
		for name, info in pairs(context.roles()) do
			if type(info) == "table" and info.Role and tostring(info.Role):lower():find(role) and info.Dead ~= true then
				return name
			end
		end
		return nil
	end
	local function lobby()
		local lobbyModel = workspace:FindFirstChild("RegularLobby") or workspace:FindFirstChild("Lobby")
		local spawn
		if lobbyModel then
			local spawns = lobbyModel:FindFirstChild("Spawns")
			spawn = (spawns and spawns:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")) or lobbyModel:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpawnLocation", true)
		end
		spawn = spawn or workspace:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpawnLocation", true)
		if not spawn then return false end
		return move(spawn.CFrame + environment.Vector3.new(0, 4, 0))
	end
	local function map()
		local current
		for _, model in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if model:IsA("Model") and not string.find(model.Name, "Lobby", 1, true)
				and (model:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer") or model:FindFirstChild("Spawns")) then
				current = model
				break
			end
		end
		if not current then
			pcall(function() context.window:Notify({Title = context.translation.tr("No map loaded"), Kind = "warning", Duration = 2.5}) end)
			return false
		end

		local points, spawns = {}, current:FindFirstChild("Spawns")
		if spawns then
			for _, part in ipairs(spawns:GetChildren()) do
				if part:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(points, part) end
			end
		end
		if #points > 0 then return move(points[math.random(1, #points)].CFrame + environment.Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)) end
		local coins = current:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer")
		local part = coins and coins:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
		if part then return move(environment.CFrame.new(part.Position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))) end
		return move(current:GetPivot() + environment.Vector3.new(0, 6, 0))
	end

	local section = context.section
	section:Columns()
	section:Group({Title = "TP Role", Side = "Left"})
	section:Button({Text = "Tp Lobby", Callback = lobby})
	section:Button({Text = "Tp Map", Callback = map})
	section:Button({Text = "Tp Murderer", Callback = function() teleportPlayer(findRole("murder")) end})
	section:Button({Text = "Tp Sheriff", Callback = function() teleportPlayer(findRole("sheriff") or findRole("hero")) end})
	section:Group({Title = "TP Player", Side = "Right"})
	section:PlayerList({ExcludeLocal = true, Momentary = true, ActionIcon = "map-pin",
		OnSelect = function(player) if player then teleportPlayer(player.Name) end end})
end

local ServerIdentity = {}

ServerIdentity.MapVote = {}

ServerIdentity.MapVote.maps = {
	{key = "bank2", name = "Bank 2", icon = "rbxassetid://3214475169"},
	{key = "biolab", name = "Bio Lab", icon = "rbxassetid://3214475802"},
	{key = "factory", name = "Factory", icon = "rbxassetid://3214476069"},
	{key = "hospital3", name = "Hospital 3", icon = "rbxassetid://3214476562"},
	{key = "hotel2", name = "Hotel 2", icon = "rbxassetid://3214477063"},
	{key = "house2", name = "House 2", icon = "rbxassetid://3214477411"},
	{key = "mansion2", name = "Mansion 2", icon = "rbxassetid://3214477676"},
	{key = "milbase", name = "Mil Base", icon = "rbxassetid://3214477899"},
	{key = "office3", name = "Office 3", icon = "rbxassetid://3214478924"},
	{key = "policestation", name = "Police Station", icon = "rbxassetid://3214479176"},
	{key = "researchfacility", name = "Research Facility", icon = "rbxassetid://4751350477"},
	{key = "workplace", name = "Workplace", icon = "rbxassetid://3214479481"},
}

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.key(name)
	return (string.lower(tostring(name or "")):gsub("[^%w]", ""))
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.create(context)
	local vote = context.configuration.vote
	if type(vote) ~= "table" then
		vote = {}
		context.configuration.vote = vote
	end
	if type(vote.count) ~= "number" then vote.count = 10 end
	if vote.panel == nil then vote.panel = true end
	if vote.silent == nil then vote.silent = true end
	if type(vote.icons) ~= "table" then vote.icons = {} end
	if type(vote.extra) ~= "table" then vote.extra = {} end
	if type(vote.off) ~= "table" then vote.off = {} end
	if vote.auto == nil then vote.auto = false end
	if type(vote.fallback) ~= "string" then vote.fallback = "skip" end

	local state = {context = context, cfg = vote, busy = false, stop = false, done = 0, want = 0,
		status = "", sel = 1, log = {}, maps = {}, mapList = {}, iconCandidates = {}}
	for _, map in ServerIdentity.MapVote.maps do
		state.maps[map.key] = map
		table.insert(state.mapList, map)
	end
	for key, extra in vote.extra do
		if type(extra) ~= "table" or state.maps[key] then continue end
		local map = {key = key, name = tostring(extra.n or key), icon = extra.i}
		state.maps[key] = map
		table.insert(state.mapList, map)
	end
	local order, seen = {}, {}
	for _, key in type(vote.order) == "table" and vote.order or {} do
		if not state.maps[key] or seen[key] then continue end
		seen[key] = true
		table.insert(order, key)
	end
	for _, map in state.mapList do
		if seen[map.key] then continue end
		seen[map.key] = true
		table.insert(order, map.key)
	end
	vote.order = order
	for key, icon in vote.icons do
		if state.maps[key] and state.maps[key].icon or type(icon) ~= "string" then
			vote.icons[key] = nil
			continue
		end
		if ServerIdentity.MapVote.keyForIcon(state, icon, key) then vote.icons[key] = nil end
	end
	return state
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.icon(state, key)
	local map = state.maps[key]
	local icon = map and map.icon or state.cfg.icons[key]
	return type(icon) == "string" and icon ~= "" and icon or nil
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.keyForIcon(state, icon, except)
	for _, map in state.mapList do
		if map.key ~= except and ServerIdentity.MapVote.icon(state, map.key) == icon then return map.key end
	end
	return nil
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.pads(workspace)
	local lobby = workspace:FindFirstChild("RegularLobby")
	local pads = lobby and lobby:FindFirstChild("VotePads")
	if pads and lobby:FindFirstChild("VotePad1") then return lobby, pads end
	for _, model in workspace:GetChildren() do
		if not model:IsA("Model") then continue end
		local folder = model:FindFirstChild("VotePads")
		if not folder then continue end
		if model:FindFirstChild("VotePad1") then return model, folder end
		if folder:FindFirstChild("VotePad1") then return folder, folder end
	end
	return nil
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.readPad(state, index)
	local parent, detectors = ServerIdentity.MapVote.pads(state.context.workspace)
	if not parent then return nil end
	local pad = parent:FindFirstChild("VotePad" .. index)
	local detector = detectors and detectors:FindFirstChild("Detector" .. index) or pad and pad:FindFirstChild("Pad")
	if not pad or not detector then return nil end
	local result = {index = index, detector = detector, name = "", mode = "", votes = 0, icon = ""}
	pcall(function()
		local container = pad.VoteInfoGui.Container
		result.name = container.MapName.Text
		result.votes = tonumber(container.Votes.Text:match("%d+")) or 0
	end)
	pcall(function()
		result.icon = pad.MapInfoGui.MapIcon.Image
		result.mode = pad.MapInfoGui.GameMode.Text
	end)
	result.active = result.icon ~= ""
	return result
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.choose(state, pads)
	local vote = state.cfg
	local ranks, rank = {}, 0
	for _, key in vote.order do
		if vote.off[key] == true then continue end
		rank += 1
		ranks[key] = rank
	end
	local chosen, bestRank
	for index = 1, 3 do
		local pad = pads[index]
		if not pad or not pad.active then continue end
		local key = ServerIdentity.MapVote.keyForIcon(state, pad.icon) or ServerIdentity.MapVote.key(pad.name)
		local currentRank = ranks[key]
		if currentRank and (not bestRank or currentRank < bestRank) then chosen, bestRank = index, currentRank end
	end
	if chosen then return chosen end
	if vote.fallback == "first" then
		for index = 1, 3 do if pads[index] and pads[index].active then return index end end
	end
	if vote.fallback == "leading" then
		local bestVotes
		for index = 1, 3 do
