			local pad = pads[index]
			if not pad or not pad.active then continue end
			if bestVotes == nil or pad.votes > bestVotes then chosen, bestVotes = index, pad.votes end
		end
	end
	return chosen
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.discover(state, name, icon, now)
	local vote = state.cfg
	local key = ServerIdentity.MapVote.key(name)
	if key == "" or name == "MAP NAME" then return false end
	local changed = false
	if not state.maps[key] then
		vote.extra[key] = {n = name, i = icon}
		local map = {key = key, name = name, icon = icon}
		state.maps[key] = map
		table.insert(state.mapList, map)
		table.insert(vote.order, key)
		changed = true
	end
	if not icon or icon == "" or state.maps[key].icon or vote.icons[key] == icon then return changed end
	if ServerIdentity.MapVote.keyForIcon(state, icon, key) then return changed end
	local candidate = state.iconCandidates[key]
	if candidate and candidate.icon == icon and now - candidate.time >= 0.4 then
		vote.icons[key] = icon
		state.iconCandidates[key] = nil
		return true
	end
	if not candidate or candidate.icon ~= icon then state.iconCandidates[key] = {icon = icon, time = now} end
	return changed
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.sortFarm(state, farm)
	if not farm or not farm.hopOf then return end
	if farm.fix then pcall(farm.fix, function(key) return state.maps[key] ~= nil end) end
	local scores, measured, available, original = {}, {}, {}, {}
	for index, key in state.cfg.order do
		local score = farm.hopOf(key)
		if score then
			scores[key] = score
			table.insert(available, score)
		end
		measured[key] = farm.measured and farm.measured(key) or false
		original[key] = index
	end
	if #available == 0 and state.cfg.explore ~= true then return end
	table.sort(available)
	local median = available[math.ceil(math.max(#available, 1) / 2)] or 0
	local order = table.clone(state.cfg.order)
	table.sort(order, function(left, right)
		if (left == "barn") ~= (right == "barn") then return right == "barn" end
		if state.cfg.explore == true and measured[left] ~= measured[right] then return not measured[left] end
		local leftScore, rightScore = scores[left] or median, scores[right] or median
		if math.abs(leftScore - rightScore) > 0.01 then return leftScore < rightScore end
		return original[left] < original[right]
	end)
	state.cfg.order = order
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.record(state, message)
	if #state.log > 60 then table.remove(state.log, 1) end
	local now = state.context.environment.os.clock()
	table.insert(state.log, string.format("%.2f %s", now, message))
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.waitCharacter(state, previous, timeout)
	local context = state.context
	local started = context.environment.os.clock()
	while context.environment.os.clock() - started < timeout do
		if state.stop then return nil end
		local character = context.player.Character
		if character and character ~= previous then
			local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if humanoid and root and humanoid.Health > 0 then return character, humanoid, root end
		end
		context.runService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end
	return nil
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.hideFades(state)
	local gui = state.context.player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
	local hidden = {}
	if not gui then return hidden end
	for _, name in {"SpawnFade", "CameraFade", "Fade"} do
		local fade = gui:FindFirstChild(name)
		if not fade or not fade:IsA("ScreenGui") or not fade.Enabled then continue end
		table.insert(hidden, fade)
		pcall(function() fade.Enabled = false end)
	end
	return hidden
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.restoreFades(state, hidden)
	if #hidden == 0 then return end
	local environment = state.context.environment
	environment.task.spawn(function()
		local started = environment.os.clock()
		while environment.os.clock() - started < 4 do
			local opaque = false
			for _, fade in hidden do
				for _, child in fade:GetChildren() do
					if child:IsA("Frame") and child.Visible and child.BackgroundTransparency < 0.98 then opaque = true end
				end
			end
			if not opaque then break end
			environment.task.wait(0.1)
		end
		for _, fade in hidden do
			pcall(function() if fade.Parent then fade.Enabled = true end end)
		end
	end)
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.protectGui(state)
	local playerGui = state.context.player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
	local gui = playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("MainGUI")
	if not gui or not gui:IsA("ScreenGui") or not gui.ResetOnSpawn then return nil end
	local ok = pcall(function() gui.ResetOnSpawn = false end)
	if not ok then return nil end
	return {gui = gui, resets = 0}
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.restoreGui(state, saved)
	if not saved or not saved.gui or not saved.gui.Parent then return end
	if saved.resets > 0 then
		state.context.environment.task.defer(function() pcall(saved.gui.Destroy, saved.gui) end)
		return
	end
	pcall(function() saved.gui.ResetOnSpawn = true end)
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.muteDeath(character)
	for _, object in character:GetDescendants() do
		if not object:IsA("Sound") or object.Name ~= "Died" and not object.Name:lower():find("death", 1, true) then continue end
		pcall(function()
			object.Volume = 0
			object:Stop()
		end)
	end
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.restoreInvisibility(state)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	environment.task.spawn(function()
		local started = environment.os.clock()
		while environment.os.clock() - started < 6 do
			local character = context.player.Character
			local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then break end
			environment.task.wait(0.1)
		end
		environment.task.wait(0.6)
		pcall(function()
			local globals = environment.getgenv()
			local getInvisible, setInvisible = globals.__pulseGetInvis, globals.__pulseSetInvis
			if setInvisible and (not getInvisible or not getInvisible()) then setInvisible(true) end
		end)
	end)
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.run(state, index, count)
	local vote = ServerIdentity.MapVote
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local settings = context.configuration
	state.busy, state.stop, state.idx, state.done, state.want, state.status = true, false, index, 0, count, "Voting"
	local fly = settings.fly and settings.fly.on
	if fly then settings.fly.on = false end
	local invisible = false
	pcall(function()
		local globals = environment.getgenv()
		if globals.__pulseGetInvis and globals.__pulseSetInvis and globals.__pulseGetInvis() then
			invisible = true
			globals.__pulseSetInvis(false)
		end
	end)
	local silent = state.cfg.silent ~= false
	local hidden = silent and vote.hideFades(state) or {}
	local gui = vote.protectGui(state)
	local ok, raised = pcall(function()
		local character, humanoid, root = vote.waitCharacter(state, nil, 15)
		local failures = 0
		while state.done < count do
			if state.stop then
				state.status = "Stopped"
				break
			end
			if not character then
				state.status = state.stop and "Stopped" or "No character"
				break
			end
			local pad = vote.readPad(state, index)
			if not pad or not pad.active then
				state.status = "Voting ended"
				break
			end
			state.status = "Voting"
			local before, started = pad.votes, environment.os.clock()
			local target = environment.CFrame.new(pad.detector.Position - environment.Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0))
			vote.record(state, "tp " .. index .. " before=" .. before)
			local counted = false
			while environment.os.clock() - started < 1.2 and not state.stop do
				local current = vote.readPad(state, index)
				if not current or not current.active then break end
				if current.votes > before and environment.os.clock() - started >= 0.18 then
					counted = true
					break
				end
				if root.Parent then
					local delta = root.Position - current.detector.Position
					if environment.Vector3.new(delta.X, 0, delta.Z).Magnitude > 1.5 or math.abs(delta.Y) > 3.5 then
						root.CFrame = target * (root.CFrame - root.CFrame.Position)
						root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = environment.Vector3.zero
					end
				end
				context.runService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			end
			vote.record(state, "vote " .. tostring(counted) .. " dt=" .. string.format("%.2f", environment.os.clock() - started))
			if counted then
				state.done += 1
				failures = 0
			end
			if not counted then
				failures += 1
				if failures >= 2 then
					state.status = "Vote not counted"
					break
				end
			end
			if state.done >= count or state.stop then break end
			state.status = "Respawning"
			if silent then pcall(vote.muteDeath, character) end
			if gui then gui.resets += 1 end
			pcall(function() humanoid.Health = 0 end)
			started = environment.os.clock()
			character, humanoid, root = vote.waitCharacter(state, character, 15)
			vote.record(state, "respawned dt=" .. string.format("%.2f", environment.os.clock() - started))
			pad = vote.readPad(state, index)
			if not pad or not pad.active then
				state.status = "Voting ended"
				break
			end
		end
		if state.done >= count then state.status = "Done" end
	end)
	if not ok then
		state.status = "Error"
		vote.record(state, "err " .. tostring(raised))
	end
	vote.restoreGui(state, gui)
	vote.restoreFades(state, hidden)
	if fly then settings.fly.on = true end
	if invisible then vote.restoreInvisibility(state) end
	state.busy = false
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.start(state, index)
	if state.busy then return end
	local pad = ServerIdentity.MapVote.readPad(state, index)
	if not pad or not pad.active then
		state.status = "Waiting for vote"
		return
	end
	state.sel = index
	local count = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(state.cfg.count) or 10), 1, 100)
	state.context.environment.task.spawn(ServerIdentity.MapVote.run, state, index, count)
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.snapshot(state)
	local vote = ServerIdentity.MapVote
	local pads, active = {}, false
	for index = 1, 3 do
		pads[index] = vote.readPad(state, index)
		if pads[index] and pads[index].active then active = true end
	end
	if active then
		local previous, complete = {}, true
		for index = 1, 3 do
			local pad = pads[index]
			previous[index] = {n = pad and pad.name or "", i = pad and pad.icon or ""}
			if previous[index].n == "" then complete = false end
		end
		if complete then state.cfg.last = previous end
		return pads, true
	end
	if type(state.cfg.last) ~= "table" then return pads, false end
	for index = 1, 3 do
		local previous = state.cfg.last[index]
		if type(previous) ~= "table" or type(previous.n) ~= "string" or previous.n == "" then continue end
		pads[index] = {active = true, idle = true, name = previous.n,
			icon = vote.icon(state, vote.key(previous.n)) or previous.i, mode = "", votes = 0}
	end
	return pads, false
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.status(state, active)
	local translate = state.context.translation.tr
	local remaining = state.startT and math.max(0, math.ceil(15 - (state.context.environment.os.clock() - state.startT)))
	local suffix = active and remaining and "  В·  " .. remaining .. "s" or ""
	if state.busy then return translate(state.status ~= "" and state.status or "Voting") .. "  " .. state.done .. "/" .. state.want .. suffix end
	if state.status ~= "" and state.idx and state.done > 0 then return translate(state.status) .. "  +" .. state.done .. suffix end
	if state.status ~= "" and state.idx and state.status ~= "Voting" then return translate(state.status) .. suffix end
	return active and translate("Voting") .. suffix or translate("Waiting for vote")
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.autoStart(state)
	if state.closed or state.autoRan or state.busy or state.cfg.auto ~= true then return end
	local vote, context = ServerIdentity.MapVote, state.context
	local clock = context.environment.os.clock
	local started, changedAt, previous = clock(), clock(), nil
	while clock() - started < 1.2 do
		local signature, identified = "", 0
		for index = 1, 3 do
			local pad = vote.readPad(state, index)
			if not pad or not pad.active then continue end
			signature ..= "|" .. pad.icon
			if vote.keyForIcon(state, pad.icon) or pad.name ~= "" and pad.name ~= "MAP NAME" then identified += 1 end
		end
		if signature ~= previous then previous, changedAt = signature, clock() end
		if identified >= 3 and clock() - changedAt >= 0.12 then break end
		context.runService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end
	if state.closed or state.autoRan or state.busy or state.cfg.auto ~= true then return end
	local pads, active = vote.snapshot(state)
	if not active then return end
	state.autoRan = true
	if state.cfg.farmSort == true then pcall(vote.sortFarm, state, rawget(context.environment.getgenv(), "__pulseMapFarm")) end
	for index = 1, 3 do
		local pad = pads[index]
		if pad and pad.active and not vote.keyForIcon(state, pad.icon) then vote.discover(state, pad.name, nil, clock()) end
	end
	local selected = vote.choose(state, pads)
	local descriptions = {}
	for index = 1, 3 do
		local pad = pads[index]
		descriptions[index] = pad and (vote.keyForIcon(state, pad.icon) or "?") .. "/" .. vote.key(pad.name) or "-"
	end
	vote.record(state, "auto pick " .. tostring(selected) .. " pads " .. table.concat(descriptions, " "))
	if selected then state.sel = selected; vote.start(state, selected) end
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.observe(state)
	local vote = ServerIdentity.MapVote
	local now = state.context.environment.os.clock()
	local pads, active = vote.snapshot(state)
	if active then
		local leadingVotes, leadingKey
		for index = 1, 3 do
			local pad = pads[index]
			if not pad or not pad.active then continue end
			if pad.name ~= "" then vote.discover(state, pad.name, pad.icon, now) end
			if leadingVotes == nil or pad.votes > leadingVotes then
				leadingVotes, leadingKey = pad.votes, vote.keyForIcon(state, pad.icon) or vote.key(pad.name)
			end
		end
		if leadingKey and leadingKey ~= "" then state.lead = leadingKey end
		if not state.wasActive then
			state.startT = now
			if not state.busy then state.idx, state.done, state.status = nil, 0, "" end
		end
		if state.cfg.auto == true and not state.autoRan and not state.busy and not state.autoPending then
			state.autoPending = true
			state.context.environment.task.spawn(function()
				pcall(vote.autoStart, state)
				state.autoPending = false
			end)
		end
	end
	if not active and state.wasActive then
		if state.lead then
			state.lastWin = {k = state.lead, t = now}
			vote.record(state, "win " .. state.lead)
		end
		state.lead, state.startT, state.autoRan = nil, nil, false
	end
	state.wasActive = active
	return pads, active
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.row(state, parent, index, compact)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local create, enum = context.create, environment.Enum
	local frame = create("TextButton", {Name = "VoteRow" .. index, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		BackgroundColor3 = environment.Color3.fromRGB(27, 30, 37), Size = compact and environment.UDim2.new(1 / 3, -4, 1, 0)
			or environment.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40), LayoutOrder = index, ZIndex = 8}, parent)
	context.corner(frame, compact and 8 or 10)
	local image = create("ImageLabel", {Name = "Map", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = "", ScaleType = enum.ScaleType.Crop,
		Position = compact and environment.UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4) or environment.UDim2.fromOffset(6, 5),
		Size = compact and environment.UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 42) or environment.UDim2.fromOffset(52, 30), ZIndex = 9}, frame)
	context.corner(image, 7)
	local title = create("TextLabel", {Name = "MapName", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", TextSize = compact and 10 or 12,
		Font = enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextColor3 = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), TextTruncate = enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
		TextXAlignment = enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Position = compact and environment.UDim2.fromOffset(4, 48) or environment.UDim2.fromOffset(66, 5),
		Size = compact and environment.UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 15) or environment.UDim2.new(1, -124, 0, 16), ZIndex = 9}, frame)
	local details = create("TextLabel", {Name = "Mode", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", TextSize = 9,
		Font = enum.Font.Gotham, TextColor3 = environment.Color3.fromRGB(138, 143, 158), TextTruncate = enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
		TextXAlignment = enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Position = compact and environment.UDim2.fromOffset(4, 65) or environment.UDim2.fromOffset(66, 21),
		Size = compact and environment.UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 14) or environment.UDim2.new(1, -124, 0, 12), ZIndex = 9}, frame)
	local votes = create("TextLabel", {Name = "Votes", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", TextSize = 11,
		Font = enum.Font.GothamBold, TextColor3 = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		AnchorPoint = environment.Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = environment.UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 5),
		Size = environment.UDim2.fromOffset(40, 14), ZIndex = 10}, frame)
	context.connect(frame.MouseButton1Click, function()
		state.sel = index
		ServerIdentity.MapVote.start(state, index)
	end)
	return {frame = frame, image = image, title = title, details = details, votes = votes}
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.rows(state, parent, compact)
	if not parent then return {} end
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local frame = context.create("Frame", {Name = "VoteList", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = compact and environment.UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 84) or environment.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 128)}, parent)
	local layout = context.create("UIListLayout", {SortOrder = environment.Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		FillDirection = compact and environment.Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal or environment.Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
		Padding = environment.UDim.new(0, compact and 6 or 4)}, frame)
	local rows = {}
	for index = 1, 3 do rows[index] = ServerIdentity.MapVote.row(state, frame, index, compact) end
	return rows, frame, layout
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.renderRows(state, rows, pads)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	for index, row in rows do
		local pad = pads[index]
		local visible = pad and pad.active
		row.title.Text = visible and pad.name ~= "" and pad.name or state.context.translation.tr("Waiting for vote")
		row.image.Image = visible and pad.icon or ""
		row.details.Text = visible and (pad.idle and "" or pad.mode) or ""
		row.votes.Text = visible and not pad.idle and tostring(pad.votes) or ""
		row.frame.BackgroundColor3 = index == state.sel and environment.Color3.fromRGB(51, 45, 55)
			or environment.Color3.fromRGB(27, 30, 37)
	end
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.priorityRow(state, parent, map)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local create, enum = context.create, environment.Enum
	local row = create("Frame", {Name = "Map_" .. map.key, Size = environment.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32),
		BackgroundColor3 = environment.Color3.fromRGB(34, 37, 45), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3}, parent)
	context.corner(row, 8)
	local rank = create("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = environment.UDim2.fromOffset(4, 0),
		Size = environment.UDim2.fromOffset(20, 32), Text = "", Font = enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 12,
		TextColor3 = environment.Color3.fromRGB(214, 68, 88), ZIndex = 4}, row)
	local image = create("ImageLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = environment.UDim2.fromOffset(28, 4),
		Size = environment.UDim2.fromOffset(44, 24), Image = "", ScaleType = enum.ScaleType.Crop, ZIndex = 4}, row)
	context.corner(image, 6)
	local label = create("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = environment.UDim2.fromOffset(80, 0),
		Size = environment.UDim2.new(1, -168, 1, 0), Text = map.name, Font = enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 12,
		TextXAlignment = enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
		TextColor3 = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), ZIndex = 4}, row)
	local function button(text, offset, callback)
		local object = create("TextButton", {AnchorPoint = environment.Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
			Position = environment.UDim2.new(1, offset, 0.5, 0), Size = environment.UDim2.fromOffset(20, 24),
			Text = text, Font = enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 14, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			TextColor3 = environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 175, 188), ZIndex = 5}, row)
		context.connect(object.MouseButton1Click, callback)
		return object
	end
	local function move(delta)
		local order = state.cfg.order
		local index = table.find(order, map.key)
		local target = index and index + delta
		if not target or target < 1 or target > #order then return end
		order[index], order[target] = order[target], order[index]
		ServerIdentity.MapVote.renderPriority(state)
	end
	local up = button("в†‘", -64, function() move(-1) end)
	local down = button("в†“", -42, function() move(1) end)
	local toggle = button("", -8, function()
		state.cfg.off[map.key] = state.cfg.off[map.key] ~= true and true or nil
		ServerIdentity.MapVote.renderPriority(state)
	end)
	return {frame = row, rank = rank, image = image, label = label, up = up, down = down, toggle = toggle}
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.renderPriority(state)
	if not state.priorityList then return end
	local vote = ServerIdentity.MapVote
	local signature = {}
	for _, key in state.cfg.order do
		table.insert(signature, key .. ":" .. tostring(state.cfg.off[key] == true) .. ":" .. tostring(vote.icon(state, key)))
	end
	signature = table.concat(signature, "|")
	if signature == state.prioritySignature then return end
	state.prioritySignature = signature
	state.priorityRows = state.priorityRows or {}
	local rank = 0
	for index, key in state.cfg.order do
		local map = state.maps[key]
		if not map then continue end
		local row = state.priorityRows[key]
		if not row then
			row = vote.priorityRow(state, state.priorityList, map)
			state.priorityRows[key] = row
		end
		local off = state.cfg.off[key] == true
		if not off then rank += 1 end
		row.frame.LayoutOrder = index
		row.rank.Text = off and "В·" or tostring(rank)
		row.image.Image = vote.icon(state, key) or ""
		row.image.ImageTransparency = off and 0.6 or 0
		row.label.TextTransparency = off and 0.5 or 0
		row.up.TextTransparency = index == 1 and 0.7 or 0
		row.down.TextTransparency = index == #state.cfg.order and 0.7 or 0
		row.toggle.Text = off and "в—‹" or "в—Џ"
	end
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.resetPriority(state)
	local order = {}
	for _, map in state.mapList do table.insert(order, map.key) end
	state.cfg.order, state.cfg.off = order, {}
	ServerIdentity.MapVote.renderPriority(state)
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.panel(state)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local window = context.window
	if not window or not window.Gui then return end
	local create, vector, dimension = context.create, environment.Vector2, environment.UDim2
	local viewport = context.workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
	local position = state.cfg.pos
	local x, y = math.floor(viewport.X / 2 - 336 * (window.OverlayScale or 1) / 2), 96
	if type(position) == "table" and tonumber(position.x) and tonumber(position.y) and position.x >= 0 and position.y >= 0
		and position.x < viewport.X - 40 and position.y < viewport.Y - 40 then x, y = position.x, position.y end
	local panel = create("Frame", {Name = "PulseVotePanel", Position = dimension.fromOffset(x, y), Size = dimension.fromOffset(336, 134),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false, ZIndex = 6}, window.Gui)
	local body = create("CanvasGroup", {Name = "Body", Size = dimension.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0.04,
		BackgroundColor3 = environment.Color3.fromRGB(27, 30, 37), GroupTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 6}, panel)
	context.corner(body, 12)
	local scale = create("UIScale", {Scale = window.OverlayScale or 1}, panel)
	local function clampPosition()
		if not panel.Parent or panel.AbsoluteSize.X <= 0 or panel.AbsoluteSize.Y <= 0 then return end
		local size, current = context.workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize, panel.Position
		local px = math.clamp(current.X.Offset, 4, math.max(4, size.X - panel.AbsoluteSize.X - 4))
		local py = math.clamp(current.Y.Offset, 4, math.max(4, size.Y - panel.AbsoluteSize.Y - 4))
		if px ~= current.X.Offset or py ~= current.Y.Offset then panel.Position = dimension.fromOffset(px, py) end
	end
	pcall(function()
		window:OnScreenScale(function(value)
			if not panel.Parent then return false end
			scale.Scale = value
			environment.task.defer(clampPosition)
		end)
	end)
	context.connect(panel:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"), function()
		if panel.Visible then environment.task.defer(clampPosition) end
	end)
	local header = create("Frame", {Name = "Head", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = dimension.new(1, 0, 0, 36),
		ZIndex = 7, Active = true}, body)
	create("TextLabel", {Text = context.translation.tr("Map Vote"), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = dimension.new(1, -175, 0, 15), Position = dimension.fromOffset(10, 4), TextSize = 12,
		Font = environment.Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextColor3 = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		TextXAlignment = environment.Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 8}, header)
	local status = create("TextLabel", {Name = "Status", Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = dimension.new(1, -175, 0, 12), Position = dimension.fromOffset(10, 19), TextSize = 9,
		Font = environment.Enum.Font.Gotham, TextColor3 = environment.Color3.fromRGB(138, 143, 158),
		TextXAlignment = environment.Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 8}, header)
	local function button(text, xOffset, width, callback)
		local object = create("TextButton", {Text = text, AnchorPoint = vector.new(1, 0), Position = dimension.new(1, xOffset, 0, 7),
			Size = dimension.fromOffset(width, 22), Font = environment.Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 11,
			BackgroundColor3 = environment.Color3.fromRGB(34, 37, 45), TextColor3 = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
			BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 9}, body)
		context.corner(object, 8)
		context.connect(object.MouseButton1Click, callback)
		return object
	end
	local stop = button(context.translation.tr("Stop"), -88, 46, function() state.stop = true end)
	button("в€’", -60, 20, function() ServerIdentity.MapVote.changeCount(state, -1) end)
	button("+", -10, 20, function() ServerIdentity.MapVote.changeCount(state, 1) end)
	local count = create("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = vector.new(1, 0), Position = dimension.new(1, -30, 0, 7),
		Size = dimension.fromOffset(30, 22), Text = tostring(state.cfg.count), Font = environment.Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 11,
		TextColor3 = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), ZIndex = 9}, body)
	local rows, rowFrame = ServerIdentity.MapVote.rows(state, body, true)
	rowFrame.Position = dimension.fromOffset(10, 40)
	state.panel, state.panelBody, state.panelRows, state.panelStatus, state.panelStop, state.panelCount = panel, body, rows, status, stop, count
	state.panelShowing = false
	local drag
	context.connect(header.InputBegan, function(input)
		local kind = input.UserInputType
		if kind ~= environment.Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and kind ~= environment.Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
		drag = {input = input, origin = input.Position, position = panel.Position}
		local connection
		connection = input.Changed:Connect(function()
			if input.UserInputState ~= environment.Enum.UserInputState.End then return end
			drag = nil
			connection:Disconnect()
			state.cfg.pos = {x = panel.Position.X.Offset, y = panel.Position.Y.Offset}
		end)
	end)
	context.connect(context.inputService.InputChanged, function(input)
		if not drag then return end
		if input.UserInputType ~= environment.Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and input ~= drag.input then return end
		local delta = input.Position - drag.origin
		local size = context.workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
		local px = math.clamp(drag.position.X.Offset + delta.X, 4, math.max(4, size.X - panel.AbsoluteSize.X - 4))
		local py = math.clamp(drag.position.Y.Offset + delta.Y, 4, math.max(4, size.Y - panel.AbsoluteSize.Y - 4))
		panel.Position = dimension.fromOffset(px, py)
	end)
	if context.applyHooks then table.insert(context.applyHooks, function()
		local saved = state.cfg.pos
		if type(saved) ~= "table" or not tonumber(saved.x) or not tonumber(saved.y) then return end
		local size = context.workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
		panel.Position = dimension.fromOffset(math.clamp(tonumber(saved.x), 0, math.max(0, size.X - 40)),
			math.clamp(tonumber(saved.y), 0, math.max(0, size.Y - 40)))
	end) end
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.panelVisibility(state, visible)
	if not state.panel or state.panelShowing == visible then return end
	state.panelShowing = visible
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	if visible then state.panel.Visible = true end
	local duration = visible and 0.28 or 0.22
	pcall(function()
		context.tweenService:Create(state.panelBody, environment.TweenInfo.new(duration), {GroupTransparency = visible and 0 or 1}):Play()
	end)
	if visible then return end
	environment.task.delay(0.24, function()
		if not state.panelShowing then pcall(function() state.panel.Visible = false end) end
	end)
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.changeCount(state, direction)
	local count = math.floor(tonumber(state.cfg.count) or 10)
	local step = (count > 10 or count == 10 and direction > 0) and 5 or 1
	state.cfg.count = math.clamp(count + direction * step, 1, 100)
	if state.countSlider then state.countSlider.Set(state.cfg.count) end
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.controls(state)
	local context, vote = state.context, ServerIdentity.MapVote
	local section = context.section
	section:Group({Title = "Map Vote", Side = "Left"})
	state.rows = vote.rows(state, section:Container(), false)
	state.countSlider = section:Slider({Text = "Votes to add", Min = 1, Max = 100, Default = state.cfg.count, Decimals = 0,
		Callback = function(value) state.cfg.count = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(value) or 10), 1, 100) end})
	section:Button({Text = "Dupe Votes", Callback = function() vote.start(state, state.sel or 1) end})
	section:Button({Text = "Stop", Callback = function() state.stop = true end})
	state.statusLabel = section:Note({Text = "Waiting for vote"})
	section:Toggle({Text = "Vote Panel", Default = state.cfg.panel ~= false, Callback = function(value) state.cfg.panel = not not value end})
	section:Toggle({Text = "Silent Respawn", Default = state.cfg.silent ~= false, Callback = function(value) state.cfg.silent = not not value end})
	section:Group({Title = "Auto Dupe", Side = "Right"})
	section:Toggle({Text = "Auto Dupe", Default = state.cfg.auto == true, Callback = function(value)
		state.cfg.auto = not not value
		if value then context.environment.task.defer(vote.autoStart, state) end
	end})
	section:Toggle({Text = "Farm Priority", Default = state.cfg.farmSort == true, Callback = function(value)
		state.cfg.farmSort = not not value
		if value then pcall(vote.sortFarm, state, rawget(context.environment.getgenv(), "__pulseMapFarm")) end
		vote.renderPriority(state)
	end})
	section:Toggle({Text = "Explore Maps", Default = state.cfg.explore == true, Callback = function(value)
		state.cfg.explore = not not value
		if state.cfg.farmSort then pcall(vote.sortFarm, state, rawget(context.environment.getgenv(), "__pulseMapFarm")) end
		vote.renderPriority(state)
	end})
	section:Dropdown({Text = "Other maps", Default = state.cfg.fallback, Options = {
		{code = "skip", label = "Don't dupe"}, {code = "leading", label = "Dupe most voted"}, {code = "first", label = "Dupe first map"}},
		Callback = function(value) state.cfg.fallback = value end})
	section:Header("Map Priority", "Right")
	local container = section:Container()
	if container then
		local environment = context.environment
		state.priorityList = context.create("Frame", {Name = "PrioList", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Size = environment.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = environment.Enum.AutomaticSize.Y}, container)
		context.create("UIListLayout", {Padding = environment.UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = environment.Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, state.priorityList)
		vote.renderPriority(state)
	end
	section:Button({Text = "Reset Priority", Callback = function() vote.resetPriority(state) end})
end

function ServerIdentity.MapVote.install(context)
	local vote = ServerIdentity.MapVote
	local state = vote.create(context)
	state.iconFor = function(name) return vote.icon(state, vote.key(name)) end
	state.isKey = function(key) return state.maps[key] ~= nil end
	state.sortFarm = function()
		vote.sortFarm(state, rawget(context.environment.getgenv(), "__pulseMapFarm"))
		vote.renderPriority(state)
	end
	context.environment.getgenv().__pulseVoteDbg = state
	vote.controls(state)
	pcall(vote.panel, state)
	context.environment.task.spawn(function()
		local lastObservation = -math.huge
		local pads, active = {}, false
		while not state.closed and context.isAlive() do
			pcall(function()
				local now = context.environment.os.clock()
				if now - lastObservation >= 0.25 then
					pads, active = vote.observe(state)
					vote.renderPriority(state)
					lastObservation = now
				end
				vote.renderRows(state, state.rows, pads)
				local status = vote.status(state, active)
				if state.statusLabel then state.statusLabel.Text = status end
				if state.panel then
					vote.panelVisibility(state, state.cfg.panel ~= false and (active or state.busy))
					state.panelStatus.Text, state.panelCount.Text = status, tostring(state.cfg.count)
					state.panelStop.Visible = state.busy
					vote.renderRows(state, state.panelRows, pads)
				end
			end)
			context.environment.task.wait(0.1)
		end
	end)
	return state, function()
		state.closed, state.stop = true, true
		if state.panel then state.panel:Destroy() end
	end
end

function ServerIdentity.install(context)
	local environment = context.environment
	local game = environment.game
	local teleport = game:GetService("TeleportService")
	local http = game:GetService("HttpService")
	local busy, status = false, nil
	local function show(message)
		pcall(function() if status then status.Text = message end end)
	end
	local function search(ascending, pages)
		local servers, cursor = {}, nil
		for _ = 1, pages do
			local url = "offline://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. game.PlaceId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder="
				.. (ascending and "Asc" or "Desc") .. "&excludeFullGames=true&limit=100" .. (cursor and "&cursor=" .. cursor or "")
			local ok, body = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(url) end)
			if not ok then break end
			local decoded, result = pcall(function() return http:JSONDecode(body) end)
			if not decoded or type(result) ~= "table" or type(result.data) ~= "table" then break end
			for _, server in ipairs(result.data) do
				if server.id and server.id ~= game.JobId and (server.playing or 0) < (server.maxPlayers or 99) then
					table.insert(servers, server)
				end
			end
			cursor = result.nextPageCursor
			if not cursor then break end
		end
		return servers
	end
	local function fail(message)
		show(context.translation.tr("Error: %s"):format(tostring(message)))
		busy = false
	end
	local function hop(small)
		if busy then return end
		busy = true
		show(context.translation.tr("Searching..."))
		environment.task.spawn(function()
			local servers = search(small, small and 1 or 3)
			if small then table.sort(servers, function(a, b) return (a.playing or 0) < (b.playing or 0) end) end
			local server = small and servers[1] or #servers > 0 and servers[math.random(1, #servers)]
			if not server then
				show(context.translation.tr("No servers found, try again"))
				busy = false
				return
			end
			local message = small and "Joining: %d/%d players" or "Hopping: %d/%d players"
			show(context.translation.tr(message):format(server.playing or 0, server.maxPlayers or 0))
			local ok, failure = pcall(function() teleport:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, server.id, context.player) end)
			if not ok then fail(failure) end
		end)
	end
	local section = context.section
	section:Button({Text = "Rejoin Server", Callback = function()
		if busy then return end
		busy = true
		show(context.translation.tr("Rejoining..."))
		environment.task.spawn(function()
			local ok, failure = pcall(function()
				if #context.players:GetPlayers() <= 1 then
					teleport:Teleport(game.PlaceId, context.player)
				else
					teleport:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, context.player)
				end
			end)
			if not ok then fail(failure) end
		end)
	end})
	section:Button({Text = "Server Hop", Callback = function() hop(false) end})
	section:Button({Text = "Join Small Server", Callback = function() hop(true) end})
	status = section:Note({Text = "Ready"})
	context.connect(teleport.TeleportInitFailed, function(_, result, message)
		busy = false
		show(context.translation.tr("Teleport failed: %s"):format(tostring(message or result)))
	end)
	section:Group({Title = "Job ID", Side = "Right"})
	section:Button({Text = "Copy Job ID", Callback = function()
		local ok = pcall(function() (environment.setclipboard or environment.toclipboard)(tostring(game.JobId)) end)
		show(ok and context.translation.tr("Copied!") or context.translation.tr("Error: %s"):format("no setclipboard"))
	end})
	if context.create then
		local row = context.create("Frame", {Size = environment.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			BorderSizePixel = 0}, section:Container())
		local background = context.create("Frame", {Size = environment.UDim2.new(1, -64, 1, 0),
			BackgroundColor3 = environment.Color3.fromRGB(18, 19, 22), BorderSizePixel = 0}, row)
		context.round(background, 6)
		background:SetAttribute("_surfL", 18 / 255)
		local input = context.create("TextBox", {Size = environment.UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, 0),
			Position = environment.UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = environment.Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
			TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = environment.Color3.fromRGB(225, 225, 230), PlaceholderText = "Job ID...",
			PlaceholderColor3 = environment.Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 118), Text = "", ClearTextOnFocus = false,
			TextXAlignment = environment.Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = environment.Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd}, background)
		local button = context.create("TextButton", {AnchorPoint = environment.Vector2.new(1, 0),
			Position = environment.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), Size = environment.UDim2.fromOffset(58, 30),
			BackgroundColor3 = context.nova.Theme.tokens.Accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.82, BorderSizePixel = 0,
			AutoButtonColor = false, Font = environment.Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 11,
			TextColor3 = environment.Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 245), Text = "Join"}, row)
		context.round(button, 6)
		context.nova.Theme.bind(button, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
		context.connect(button.MouseButton1Click, function()
			local job = tostring(input.Text or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
			if job == "" then
				show(context.translation.tr("Paste Job ID first"))
				return
			end
			if job == tostring(game.JobId) then
				show(context.translation.tr("You are already on this server"))
				return
			end
			if busy then return end
			busy = true
			show(context.translation.tr("Joining..."))
			environment.task.spawn(function()
				local ok, failure = pcall(function() teleport:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, job, context.player) end)
				if not ok then fail(failure) end
			end)
		end)
	end
	section:Group({Title = "Server Info", Side = "Right"})
	local info = section:Note({Text = "..."})
	environment.task.spawn(function()
		while context.isAlive() do
			pcall(function()
				local ping = 0
				pcall(function() ping = math.floor(context.player:GetNetworkPing() * 1000 + 0.5) end)
				info.Text = context.translation.tr("Players: %d/%d Ping: %d ms")
					:format(#context.players:GetPlayers(), context.players.MaxPlayers, ping) .. "\nJob: " .. tostring(game.JobId)
			end)
			environment.task.wait(2)
		end
	end)
end

function ServerIdentity.read(environment)
	local readers = {
		function() return environment.gethiddenproperty(environment.game, "JobId") end,
		function() return environment.game.JobId end,
		function() return environment.cloneref(environment.game).JobId end,
	}

	for _, read in environment.ipairs(readers) do
		local ok, value = environment.pcall(read)

		if ok and environment.type(value) == "string"
			and value:match("^%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x$") then
			return environment.string.lower(value)
		end
	end

	local ok, value = environment.pcall(function()
		return environment.game.JobId
	end)

	if ok then
		return environment.tostring(value or "") or ""
	end

	return ""
end

local NovaUtility = {}

local TEXT_CLASSES = {TextLabel = true, TextButton = true, TextBox = true}

function NovaUtility.create(environment, windows, connections, services)
	local registries = {}
	local detached = {}
	local utility = {radiusMult = 1, textScale = 1}

	function utility.newReg()
		local registry = {}
		table.insert(registries, registry)
		return registry
	end

	function utility.sweep()
		local entries = {}

		for _, registry in registries do
			for object in registry do
				table.insert(entries, {object = object, registry = registry})
			end
		end

		local parents = {}
		local started = environment.os.clock()

		for _, entry in entries do
			local object, registry = entry.object, entry.registry

			if registry[object] ~= nil then
				NovaUtility._sweepEntry(environment, object, registry, parents, detached)
			end

			if environment.os.clock() - started > 0.001 then
				environment.task.wait()
				started = environment.os.clock()
				parents = {}
			end
		end
	end

	function utility.regCount()
		local count = 0

		for _, registry in registries do
			for _ in registry do
				count += 1
			end
		end

		return count
	end

	function utility.startSweep()
		if utility.sweeping then
			return
		end

		utility.sweeping = true
		environment.task.spawn(function()
			while true do
				environment.task.wait(15)
				local hasWindows = false

				for _, window in environment.ipairs(windows) do
					if not window.Destroyed then
						hasWindows = true
					end
				end

				utility.sweep()

				if not hasWindows then
					utility.sweeping = false
					return
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	utility.textReg = utility.newReg()
	utility.cornerReg = utility.newReg()

	function utility.new(className, properties, children)
		local object = environment.Instance.new(className)
		local parent

		for key, value in environment.pairs(properties or {}) do
			if key == "Parent" then
				parent = value
				continue
			end

			object[key] = value
		end

		for _, child in environment.ipairs(children or {}) do
			child.Parent = object
		end

		if TEXT_CLASSES[className] then
			local font = object.FontFace
			local size = object.TextSize
			utility.textReg[object] = {weight = font.Weight, style = font.Style, size = size}

			if utility.textScale ~= 1 then
				object.TextSize = environment.math.max(8, environment.math.floor(size * utility.textScale + 0.5))
			end
		end

		if className == "UICorner" then
			local radius = object.CornerRadius
			utility.cornerReg[object] = radius

			if utility.radiusMult ~= 1 and radius.Scale == 0 then
				object.CornerRadius = environment.UDim.new(0, environment.math.floor(radius.Offset * utility.radiusMult + 0.5))
			end
		end

		if parent then
			object.Parent = parent
		end

		return object
	end

	function utility.setTextSize(object, size)
		local entry = utility.textReg[object]

		if entry then
			entry.size = size
		end

		object.TextSize = environment.math.max(8, environment.math.floor(size * utility.textScale + 0.5))
	end

	function utility.corner(parent, radius)
		local value = environment.typeof(radius) == "UDim" and radius or environment.UDim.new(0, radius or 8)
		return utility.new("UICorner", {CornerRadius = value, Parent = parent})
	end

	function utility.radiusOne(object)
		local radius = utility.cornerReg[object]

		if radius and radius.Scale == 0 then
			object.CornerRadius = environment.UDim.new(0, environment.math.floor(radius.Offset * utility.radiusMult + 0.5))
		end
	end

	function utility.connect(event, callback, registry)
		local connection = event:Connect(callback)
		table.insert(registry or connections, connection)
		return connection
	end

	function utility.leave(object, callback)
		local connection = object.MouseLeave:Connect(callback)
		object.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == environment.Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				environment.task.delay(0.12, callback)
			end
		end)
		return connection
	end

	function utility.setRadius(radius)
		utility.radiusMult = environment.math.clamp(radius, 0, 2)
		services.refresh.run("radius", utility.radiusOne, function()
			for object in utility.cornerReg do
				if object.Parent then
					utility.radiusOne(object)
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	function utility.stroke(parent, color, thickness, transparency, mode)
		return utility.new("UIStroke", {
			Color = color or environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Thickness = thickness or 1,
			Transparency = transparency or 0, ApplyStrokeMode = mode or environment.Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
			Parent = parent,
		})
	end

	function utility.pad(parent, top, right, bottom, left)
		return utility.new("UIPadding", {
			PaddingTop = environment.UDim.new(0, top or 0),
			PaddingRight = environment.UDim.new(0, right or top or 0),
			PaddingBottom = environment.UDim.new(0, bottom or top or 0),
			PaddingLeft = environment.UDim.new(0, left or right or top or 0), Parent = parent,
		})
	end

	function utility.list(parent, padding, direction, horizontal, vertical)
		return utility.new("UIListLayout", {
			Padding = environment.UDim.new(0, padding or 0),
			FillDirection = direction or environment.Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
			HorizontalAlignment = horizontal or environment.Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left,
			VerticalAlignment = vertical or environment.Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top,
			SortOrder = environment.Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = parent,
		})
	end

	function utility.shadow(parent, transparency, blur, offset, spread)
		local ok, shadow = environment.pcall(function()
			return utility.new("UIShadow", {
				Color = environment.Color3.new(0, 0, 0), Transparency = transparency or 0.5,
				BlurRadius = environment.UDim.new(0, blur or 12), Offset = environment.UDim2.fromOffset(0, offset or 2),
				Spread = environment.UDim2.fromOffset(spread or 0, spread or 0), Parent = parent,
			})
		end)

		return ok and shadow or nil
	end

	function utility.clamp(value, minimum, maximum)
		if value < minimum then
			return minimum
		end

		if value > maximum then
			return maximum
		end

		return value
	end

	function utility.round(value, decimals)
		local multiplier = 10 ^ (decimals or 0)
		return environment.math.floor(value * multiplier + 0.5) / multiplier
	end

	function utility.fmt(value, decimals)
		decimals = decimals or 0

		if decimals <= 0 then
			return environment.tostring(environment.math.floor(value + 0.5))
		end

		return environment.string.format("%." .. decimals .. "f", value)
	end

	function utility.textWidth(text, font, size)
		local ok, bounds = environment.pcall(function()
			local params = environment.Instance.new("GetTextBoundsParams")
			params.Text = environment.tostring(text)
			params.Font = font
			params.Size = size
			params.Width = 10000
			return services.textService:GetTextBoundsAsync(params)
		end)

		if ok and bounds then
			return bounds.X
		end

		return #environment.tostring(text) * size * 0.5
	end

	function utility.hostGui()
		local gui

		environment.pcall(function()
			if environment.gethui then
				gui = environment.gethui()
			end
		end)

		if environment.typeof(gui) ~= "Instance" then
			environment.pcall(function()
				gui = environment.game:GetService("CoreGui")
			end)
			local ok = environment.pcall(function()
				local folder = environment.Instance.new("Folder")
				folder.Parent = gui
				folder:Destroy()
			end)

			if not ok then
				gui = nil
			end
		end

		if environment.typeof(gui) ~= "Instance" then
			gui = services.player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
		end

		return gui
	end

	function utility.protect(gui)
		environment.pcall(function()
			if environment.syn and environment.syn.protect_gui then
				environment.syn.protect_gui(gui)
			end
		end)
		environment.pcall(function()
			if environment.protectgui then
				environment.protectgui(gui)
			end
		end)
	end

	function utility.lighten(color, amount)
		local hue, saturation, value = color:ToHSV()
		return environment.Color3.fromHSV(hue, environment.math.max(0, saturation - amount * 0.35),
			environment.math.min(1, value + amount))
	end

	function utility.darken(color, amount)
		local hue, saturation, value = color:ToHSV()
		return environment.Color3.fromHSV(hue, saturation, environment.math.max(0, value - amount))
	end

	function utility.mouse()
		return services.inputService:GetMouseLocation() - environment.game:GetService("GuiService"):GetGuiInset()
	end

	function utility.isClick(input)
		return input.UserInputType == environment.Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			or input.UserInputType == environment.Enum.UserInputType.Touch
	end

	function utility.isMove(input)
		return input.UserInputType == environment.Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
			or input.UserInputType == environment.Enum.UserInputType.Touch
	end

	function utility.sameMove(input, started)
		if started and started.UserInputType == environment.Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			return input == started
		end

		if started then
			return input.UserInputType == environment.Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
		end

		return utility.isMove(input)
	end

	function utility.sameEnd(input, started)
		if started and started.UserInputType == environment.Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			return input == started
		end

		return utility.isClick(input)
	end

	function utility.at(input)
		if input and input.UserInputType == environment.Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			return environment.Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
		end

		return utility.mouse()
	end

	return utility
end

function NovaUtility._sweepEntry(environment, object, registry, parents, detached)
	local ok, attached = environment.pcall(NovaUtility._isAttached, object, parents)

	if ok and attached then
		detached[object] = nil
		return
	end

	if detached[object] then
		registry[object] = nil
		detached[object] = nil
		return
	end

	detached[object] = true
end

function NovaUtility._isAttached(object, parents)
	local parent = object.Parent

	if parent == nil then
		return false
	end

	local attached = parents[parent]

	if attached == nil then
		attached = object:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("ScreenGui") ~= nil
		parents[parent] = attached
	end

	return attached
end

local NovaMotion = {}

function NovaMotion.create(environment, tweenService, utility)
	local easing = environment.Enum.EasingStyle
	local direction = environment.Enum.EasingDirection
	local tweenInfo = environment.TweenInfo.new
	local motion = {
		enabled = true,
		Presets = {
			Quick = tweenInfo(0.15, easing.Quint, direction.Out),
			Base = tweenInfo(0.25, easing.Quint, direction.Out),
			Slow = tweenInfo(0.4, easing.Quint, direction.Out),
			Pop = tweenInfo(0.35, easing.Back, direction.Out),
			PopSmall = tweenInfo(0.22, easing.Back, direction.Out),
			Exit = tweenInfo(0.18, easing.Quint, direction.In),
			Linear = tweenInfo(0.2, easing.Linear),
			Spin = tweenInfo(1.2, easing.Quint, direction.InOut, -1),
		},
	}

	function motion.tween(object, preset, properties)
		if environment.typeof(object) ~= "Instance" then
			return nil
		end

		if environment.type(preset) == "string" then
			preset = motion.Presets[preset] or motion.Presets.Base
		end

		if motion.enabled then
			local ok, tween = environment.pcall(tweenService.Create, tweenService, object, preset, properties)

			if ok and tween then
				tween:Play()
				return tween
			end
		end

		for property, value in environment.pairs(properties) do
			environment.pcall(function()
				object[property] = value
			end)
		end

		return nil
	end

	function motion.ripple(object, color, usePointer)
		if not motion.enabled or environment.typeof(object) ~= "Instance" then
			return
		end

		local position = object.AbsolutePosition
		local size = object.AbsoluteSize
		local origin = size / 2

		if usePointer then
			local mouse = utility.mouse()
			origin = environment.Vector2.new(
				utility.clamp(mouse.X - position.X, 0, size.X),
				utility.clamp(mouse.Y - position.Y, 0, size.Y)
			)
		end

		local diameter = environment.math.max(size.X, size.Y) * 1.6
		local ripple = utility.new("Frame", {
			Name = "Ripple", AnchorPoint = environment.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
			Position = environment.UDim2.fromOffset(origin.X, origin.Y), Size = environment.UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4),
			BackgroundColor3 = color or environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
			BackgroundTransparency = 0.8, ZIndex = object.ZIndex + 5, Parent = object,
		})

		utility.corner(ripple, environment.UDim.new(1, 0))
		motion.tween(ripple, tweenInfo(0.45, easing.Quad, direction.Out), {
			Size = environment.UDim2.fromOffset(diameter, diameter), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		})
		environment.task.delay(0.5, function()
			ripple:Destroy()
		end)
	end

	return motion
end

local NovaRefresh = {}

function NovaRefresh.create(environment, windows)
	local refresh = {gen = 0, names = {}, full = {}, busy = 0, budget = 0.003}

	function refresh.run(name, apply, fallback)
		refresh.gen += 1
		refresh.names[name] = (refresh.names[name] or 0) + 1
		local generation = refresh.names[name]
		local roots = {}

		for _, window in environment.ipairs(windows or {}) do
			if not window.Destroyed and window.Gui and window.Gui.Parent then
				table.insert(roots, window.Gui)
			end
		end

		if #roots == 0 then
			if fallback then
				fallback()
			end

			return
		end

		refresh.busy += 1
		environment.task.spawn(function()
			local started = environment.os.clock()

			for _, root in environment.ipairs(roots) do
				local objects = root:GetDescendants()

				for index = 1, #objects do
					if refresh.names[name] ~= generation then
						refresh.busy -= 1
						return
					end

					apply(objects[index])

					if index % 40 == 0 and environment.os.clock() - started > refresh.budget then
						environment.task.wait()
						started = environment.os.clock()
					end
				end
			end

			refresh.busy -= 1
		end)
	end

	function refresh.sync(root)
		if not root then
			return
		end

		local objects = root:GetDescendants()
		objects[#objects + 1] = root

		for _, apply in environment.ipairs(refresh.full) do
			for _, object in environment.ipairs(objects) do
				apply(object)
			end
		end
	end

	return refresh
end

local NovaTheme = {}

local NOVA_BASES = {
	{"Midnight", {Background = "0c0d10", Sidebar = "0f1014", Surface = "14161b", Surface2 = "1b1e25",
		Elevated = "22252d", Line = "1f2229", Text = "eceef3", Muted = "8a8f9e", Faint = "575c69"}},
	{"Graphite", {Background = "111214", Sidebar = "141517", Surface = "1a1b1e", Surface2 = "232427",
		Elevated = "2b2d31", Line = "2a2c30", Text = "f0f1f3", Muted = "9a9da4", Faint = "62656c"}},
	{"Amoled", {Background = "000000", Sidebar = "040405", Surface = "0b0c0e", Surface2 = "111216",
		Elevated = "181a1f", Line = "16181c", Text = "f2f3f6", Muted = "8f94a1", Faint = "545965"}},
	{"Deep Sea", {Background = "090d13", Sidebar = "0b1119", Surface = "101823", Surface2 = "162131",
		Elevated = "1d2b3e", Line = "1a2433", Text = "e8eef8", Muted = "8794ab", Faint = "55627a"}},
	{"Plum", {Background = "0e0b12", Sidebar = "110d16", Surface = "17121d", Surface2 = "1f1927",
		Elevated = "271f32", Line = "221b2b", Text = "efe9f6", Muted = "9a8fa8", Faint = "625870"}},
	{"Forest", {Background = "0b0f0d", Sidebar = "0d1210", Surface = "121815", Surface2 = "19211d",
		Elevated = "202a25", Line = "1c241f", Text = "e9f0ec", Muted = "8a9a92", Faint = "56645d"}},
	{"Halloween", {Background = "0d0b0a", Sidebar = "100e0c", Surface = "161311", Surface2 = "1e1a17",
		Elevated = "27221e", Line = "241f1b", Text = "f5eee6", Muted = "a39a90", Faint = "675e55"}},
	-- MYTHIC: fundo preto, menus roxo profundo, realces vermelhos.
	{"Mythic", {Background = "09070d", Sidebar = "140b20", Surface = "1b1028", Surface2 = "241431",
		Elevated = "301a41", Line = "43224f", Text = "f7efff", Muted = "b9a3c9", Faint = "7a668a"}},
}

function NovaTheme.create(environment, utility, refresh)
	local fromHex = environment.Color3.fromHex
	local bases = {}

	for _, base in NOVA_BASES do
		table.insert(bases, {base[1], table.clone(base[2])})
	end

	local theme = {
		registry = utility.newReg(), listeners = {}, Bases = bases, base = "Midnight",
		Presets = {
			Crimson = fromHex("d64458"), Ember = fromHex("ff7a3d"), Gold = fromHex("f5b94a"),
			Mint = fromHex("3ddc97"), Ocean = fromHex("3d9bff"), Violet = fromHex("8b6cff"),
			Rose = fromHex("ff5fa2"), Ice = fromHex("7fe0ff"),
		},
	}

	theme.tokens = {
		Background = fromHex("0c0d10"), Sidebar = fromHex("0f1014"), Surface = fromHex("14161b"),
		Surface2 = fromHex("1b1e25"), Elevated = fromHex("22252d"), Line = fromHex("1f2229"),
		Text = fromHex("eceef3"), Muted = fromHex("8a8f9e"), Faint = fromHex("575c69"),
		Accent = theme.Presets.Crimson, OnAccent = fromHex("ffffff"), Success = fromHex("3ddc97"),
		Warning = fromHex("f5b94a"), Danger = fromHex("f0506e"), Glass = fromHex("ffffff"),
	}

	function theme.resolve(role)
		if environment.typeof(role) == "Color3" then
			return role
		end

		if environment.type(role) == "function" then
			return role(theme.tokens)
		end

		return theme.tokens[role] or theme.tokens.Text
	end

	function theme.bind(object, property, role)
		if environment.typeof(object) ~= "Instance" then
			return object
		end

		local properties = theme.registry[object]

		if not properties then
			properties = {}
			theme.registry[object] = properties
		end

		properties[property] = role
		environment.pcall(function()
			object[property] = theme.resolve(role)
		end)
		return object
	end

	function theme.applyOne(object, filter)
		local properties = theme.registry[object]

		if not properties then
			return
		end

		for property, role in environment.pairs(properties) do
			if not filter or filter(role) then
				local ok, value = environment.pcall(theme.resolve, role)

				if ok and value ~= nil then
					environment.pcall(function()
						object[property] = value
					end)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function apply(filter, name)
		refresh.run("theme:" .. environment.tostring(name or "all"), function(object)
			theme.applyOne(object, filter)
		end, function()
			for object in environment.pairs(theme.registry) do
				if object.Parent then
					theme.applyOne(object, filter)
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	function theme.set(role, color)
		if environment.typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then
			return
		end

		theme.tokens[role] = color
		apply(function(value) return value == role or environment.type(value) == "function" end, "one:" .. environment.tostring(role))

		for _, listener in environment.ipairs(theme.listeners) do
			environment.pcall(listener, role, color)
		end
	end

	function theme.setMany(colors)
		for role, color in environment.pairs(colors) do
			if environment.typeof(color) == "Color3" then
				theme.tokens[role] = color
			end
		end

		local names = {}

		for role in environment.pairs(colors) do
			table.insert(names, environment.tostring(role))
		end

		table.sort(names)
		apply(function(role)
			return environment.type(role) == "function" or environment.type(role) == "string" and colors[role] ~= nil
		end, "many:" .. table.concat(names, ","))

		for _, listener in environment.ipairs(theme.listeners) do
			for role, color in environment.pairs(colors) do
				environment.pcall(listener, role, color)
			end
		end
	end

	function theme.setBase(name)
		for _, base in environment.ipairs(theme.Bases) do
			if base[1] == name then
				local colors = {}

				for role, value in environment.pairs(base[2]) do
					colors[role] = fromHex(value)
				end

				theme.setMany(colors)
				theme.base = name
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	function theme.panelFrom(color)
		local hue, saturation, value = color:ToHSV()
		saturation = environment.math.min(saturation, 0.9)
		value = environment.math.clamp(value, 0.025, 0.3)

		local function panel(offset, multiplier)
			return environment.Color3.fromHSV(hue, environment.math.clamp(saturation * (multiplier or 1), 0, 1),
				environment.math.clamp(value + offset, 0, 1))
		end

		return {
			Background = panel(0), Sidebar = panel(0.014), Surface = panel(0.042), Surface2 = panel(0.08, 0.92),
			Elevated = panel(0.112, 0.86), Line = panel(0.096, 0.9),
			Text = environment.Color3.fromHSV(hue, saturation * 0.07, 0.95),
			Muted = environment.Color3.fromHSV(hue, saturation * 0.16, 0.62),
			Faint = environment.Color3.fromHSV(hue, saturation * 0.18, 0.41),
		}
	end

	function theme.onChange(listener)
		table.insert(theme.listeners, listener)
	end

	function theme.accentSoft(amount)
		return function(tokens) return tokens.Surface2:Lerp(tokens.Accent, amount) end
	end

	return theme
end

local NovaFont = {}

function NovaFont.create(environment, utility, refresh)
	local cache = {}
	local font = {
		name = "Builder Sans", id = "BuilderSans",
		Families = {
			{"Builder Sans", "BuilderSans"}, {"Montserrat", "Montserrat"}, {"Nunito", "Nunito"},
			{"Roboto", "Roboto"}, {"Ubuntu", "Ubuntu"}, {"Titillium Web", "TitilliumWeb"},
			{"Josefin Sans", "JosefinSans"}, {"Jura", "Jura"}, {"Source Sans", "SourceSansPro"},
			{"Oswald", "Oswald"}, {"Roboto Mono", "RobotoMono"}, {"Michroma", "Michroma"},
		},
	}

	function font.get(weight, style)
		weight = weight or environment.Enum.FontWeight.Medium
		style = style or environment.Enum.FontStyle.Normal
		local key = font.id .. "|" .. weight.Name .. "|" .. style.Name

		if not cache[key] then
			local path = "rbxasset://fonts/families/" .. font.id .. ".json"
			local ok, value = environment.pcall(environment.Font.new, path, weight, style)
			cache[key] = ok and value or environment.Font.fromEnum(environment.Enum.Font.GothamMedium)
		end

		return cache[key]
	end

	function font.Regular() return font.get(environment.Enum.FontWeight.Regular) end
	function font.Medium() return font.get(environment.Enum.FontWeight.Medium) end
	function font.Semi() return font.get(environment.Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold) end
	function font.Bold() return font.get(environment.Enum.FontWeight.Bold) end
	function font.Heavy() return font.get(environment.Enum.FontWeight.ExtraBold) end

	function font.fontOne(object)
		local settings = utility.textReg[object]

		if settings then
			environment.pcall(function()
				object.FontFace = font.get(settings.weight, settings.style)
			end)
		end
	end

	function font.sizeOne(object)
		local settings = utility.textReg[object]

		if settings then
			environment.pcall(function()
				object.TextSize = environment.math.max(8, environment.math.floor(settings.size * utility.textScale + 0.5))
			end)
		end
	end

	function font.setFamily(name)
		for _, family in environment.ipairs(font.Families) do
			if family[1] == name or family[2] == name then
				font.name = family[1]
				font.id = family[2]
				refresh.run("font", font.fontOne, function()
					for object in environment.pairs(utility.textReg) do
						if object.Parent then
							font.fontOne(object)
						end
					end
				end)
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	function font.setScale(scale)
		utility.textScale = environment.math.clamp(scale, 0.8, 1.3)
		refresh.run("textsize", font.sizeOne, function()
			for object in environment.pairs(utility.textReg) do
				if object.Parent then
					font.sizeOne(object)
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	return font
end

local ICON_URLS = {"rbxassetid://104502745253902", "rbxassetid://89421818506275"}
local ICON_ALIASES = {
	["trash-2"] = "trash", gear = "settings", grid = "grid-3x3", ["person-running"] = "footprints",
	["alert-triangle"] = "triangle-alert", ["alert-circle"] = "circle-alert", ["check-circle"] = "circle-check",
	["check-circle-2"] = "circle-check-big", ["x-circle"] = "circle-x", ["help-circle"] = "circle-question-mark",
	["more-horizontal"] = "ellipsis", ["more-vertical"] = "ellipsis-vertical", edit = "pencil", ["edit-2"] = "pencil",
	["edit-3"] = "pencil-line", home = "house", unlock = "lock-open", ["bar-chart"] = "chart-bar",
	["bar-chart-2"] = "chart-bar", ["loader-2"] = "loader-circle", sliders = "sliders-horizontal", filter = "funnel",
	["wand-2"] = "wand-sparkles", ["play-circle"] = "circle-play", ["pause-circle"] = "circle-pause",
	["stop-circle"] = "circle-stop", ["user-circle"] = "circle-user", ["plus-circle"] = "circle-plus",
	["minus-circle"] = "circle-minus", ["pie-chart"] = "chart-pie", ["line-chart"] = "chart-line",
	["shield-close"] = "shield-x", ["sort-asc"] = "arrow-up-narrow-wide", ["sort-desc"] = "arrow-down-wide-narrow",
	verified = "badge-check", ["file-warning"] = "file-exclamation-point",
}

function NovaIcons.create(environment)
	local icons = {module = nil, tried = false, waiting = {}}
	local size = environment.Vector2.new(24, 24)

	function icons.load()
		if icons.module then
			return icons.module
		end

		icons.tried = true
		local indexes = {}

		for index, name in environment.ipairs(environment.string.split(ICON_NAMES, ",")) do
			indexes[name] = index
		end

		local positions = environment.string.split(ICON_POSITIONS, ";")
		icons.module = {GetAsset = function(name)
			local index = indexes[name] or indexes[ICON_ALIASES[name] or ""]
			local position = index and positions[index]

			if not position then
				return nil
			end

			local values = environment.string.split(position, ",")
			return {
				IconName = name, Url = ICON_URLS[environment.tonumber(values[1])], ImageRectSize = size,
				ImageRectOffset = environment.Vector2.new(environment.tonumber(values[2]), environment.tonumber(values[3])),
			}
		end}

		local waiting = icons.waiting
		icons.waiting = {}

		for _, callback in environment.ipairs(waiting) do
			environment.pcall(callback)
		end

		return icons.module
	end

	function icons.resolve(name)
		if not name or name == "" then
			return nil
		end

		if environment.type(name) == "number" or environment.tostring(name):match("^%d+$") then
			return {Url = "rbxassetid://" .. environment.tostring(name),
				ImageRectOffset = environment.Vector2.zero, ImageRectSize = environment.Vector2.zero}
		end

		if environment.tostring(name):match("^rbx") then
			return {Url = environment.tostring(name), ImageRectOffset = environment.Vector2.zero,
				ImageRectSize = environment.Vector2.zero}
		end

		if not icons.module then
			icons.load()
		end

		if not icons.module then
			return nil
		end

		local ok, asset = environment.pcall(icons.module.GetAsset, name)

		if ok and environment.type(asset) == "table" then
			return asset
		end

		return nil
	end

	function icons.apply(object, name)
		if environment.typeof(object) ~= "Instance" then
			return
		end

		if not icons.module then
			icons.load()
		end

		local asset = icons.resolve(name)

		if asset then
			object.Image = asset.Url
			object.ImageRectOffset = asset.ImageRectOffset or environment.Vector2.zero
			object.ImageRectSize = asset.ImageRectSize or environment.Vector2.zero
			object.Visible = true
		end
	end

	return icons
end

end -- NovaIcons private scope

local NovaStore = {}

function NovaStore.create(environment, json)
	local store = {}

	function store.new(path)
		local state = {path = path, data = {}, dirty = false}

		environment.pcall(function()
			if path and environment.isfile and environment.readfile and environment.isfile(path) then
				local data = json:JSONDecode(environment.readfile(path))

				if environment.type(data) == "table" then
					state.data = data
				end
			end
		end)

		function state.get(key, default)
			local value = state.data[key]

			if value == nil then
				return default
			end

			return value
		end

		function state.set(key, value)
			if state.data[key] == value then
				return
			end

			state.data[key] = value
			state.save()
		end

		function state.save()
			if state.dirty then
				return
			end

			state.dirty = true
			environment.task.delay(0.6, function()
				state.dirty = false
				environment.pcall(function()
					if not path or not environment.writefile then
						return
					end

					if environment.isfolder and environment.makefolder then
						if not environment.isfolder("PulseHub") then
							environment.makefolder("PulseHub")
						end

						if not environment.isfolder("PulseHub/nova") then
							environment.makefolder("PulseHub/nova")
						end
					end

					environment.writefile(path, json:JSONEncode(state.data))
				end)
			end)
		end

		return state
	end

	return store
end

local NovaSound = {}

function NovaSound.create(environment)
	local sound = {
		volume = 0.5, enabled = true, cache = {}, folder = "PulseHub/nova",
		specs = {
			click = {dur = 0.07, tones = {{f0 = 1900, f1 = 1300, decay = 70, amp = 0.55},
				{f0 = 3800, f1 = 2600, decay = 120, amp = 0.18}}, noise = 0.05},
			hover = {dur = 0.035, tones = {{f0 = 2600, f1 = 2400, decay = 160, amp = 0.22}}},
			on = {dur = 0.12, tones = {{f0 = 880, f1 = 880, decay = 40, amp = 0.4},
				{f0 = 1320, f1 = 1320, decay = 40, amp = 0.4, start = 0.045}}},
			off = {dur = 0.12, tones = {{f0 = 1320, f1 = 1320, decay = 40, amp = 0.35},
				{f0 = 880, f1 = 880, decay = 40, amp = 0.35, start = 0.045}}},
			open = {dur = 0.22, tones = {{f0 = 520, f1 = 980, glide = 0.5, decay = 16, amp = 0.35},
				{f0 = 1040, f1 = 1960, glide = 0.5, decay = 22, amp = 0.12}}},
			close = {dur = 0.18, tones = {{f0 = 980, f1 = 520, glide = 0.6, decay = 20, amp = 0.3}}},
			notify = {dur = 0.45, tones = {{f0 = 1175, f1 = 1175, decay = 9, amp = 0.3},
				{f0 = 1568, f1 = 1568, decay = 9, amp = 0.28, start = 0.09}}},
			tick = {dur = 0.025, tones = {{f0 = 3200, f1 = 3000, decay = 220, amp = 0.16}}},
		},
	}

	function sound.asset(name)
		if sound.cache[name] ~= nil then
			return sound.cache[name]
		end

		sound.cache[name] = false
		local ok, asset = environment.pcall(function()
			if not environment.writefile or not environment.getcustomasset then
				return nil
			end

			if environment.isfolder and environment.makefolder then
				if not environment.isfolder("PulseHub") then
					environment.makefolder("PulseHub")
				end

				if not environment.isfolder(sound.folder) then
					environment.makefolder(sound.folder)
				end
			end

			local path = sound.folder .. "/" .. name .. ".wav"

			if not (environment.isfile and environment.isfile(path)) then
				local samples = NovaSound._samples(environment, sound.specs[name])
				environment.writefile(path, NovaSound._wave(environment, samples))
			end

			return environment.getcustomasset(path)
		end)

		if ok and asset then
			sound.cache[name] = asset
		end

		return sound.cache[name]
	end

	function sound.play(name, volume)
		if not sound.enabled or sound.volume <= 0 then
			return
		end

		local asset = sound.asset(name)

		if not asset or not sound.host then
			return
		end

		local instance = environment.Instance.new("Sound")
		instance.SoundId = asset
		instance.Volume = sound.volume * (volume or 1)
		instance.Parent = sound.host
		instance:Play()

		environment.task.delay(1, function()
			instance:Destroy()
		end)
	end

	function sound.preload()
		environment.task.spawn(function()
			for name in environment.pairs(sound.specs) do
				environment.pcall(sound.asset, name)
			end
		end)
	end

	return sound
end

function NovaSound._samples(environment, specification)
	local count = environment.math.floor(22050 * specification.dur)
	local samples = table.create(count)
	local phases = {}

	for index = 1, count do
		local time = (index - 1) / 22050
		local progress = time / specification.dur
		local value = 0

		for toneIndex, tone in environment.ipairs(specification.tones) do
			local frequency = tone.f0 + (tone.f1 - tone.f0) * environment.math.min(1, progress / (tone.glide or 1))
			phases[toneIndex] = (phases[toneIndex] or 0) + 6.283185307179586 * frequency / 22050
			local envelope = environment.math.exp(-time * (tone.decay or 30))
				* environment.math.min(1, time / (tone.attack or 0.002))

			if tone.start and time < tone.start then
				envelope = 0
			end

			if tone.start and time >= tone.start then
				local elapsed = time - tone.start
				envelope = environment.math.exp(-elapsed * (tone.decay or 30))
					* environment.math.min(1, elapsed / (tone.attack or 0.002))
			end

			value += environment.math.sin(phases[toneIndex]) * envelope * (tone.amp or 0.5)
		end

		if specification.noise then
			value += (environment.math.random() * 2 - 1) * specification.noise * environment.math.exp(-time * 90)
		end

		samples[index] = value
	end

	return samples
end

function NovaSound._wave(environment, samples)
	local count = #samples
	local header = {
		"RIFF", environment.string.pack("<I4", 36 + count * 2), "WAVE", "fmt ",
		environment.string.pack("<I4I2I2I4I4I2I2", 16, 1, 1, 22050, 44100, 2, 16),
		"data", environment.string.pack("<I4", count * 2),
	}
	local encoded = table.create(count)

	for index, sample in samples do
		local value = environment.math.floor(environment.math.clamp(sample, -1, 1) * 32767)
		encoded[index] = environment.string.pack("<i2", value)
	end

	return table.concat(header) .. table.concat(encoded)
end

local NovaTexture = {}

do -- Keep module-private locals out of the main chunk's register budget.

local CRC32_TABLE
local TEXTURE_BUCKETS = {3, 5, 7, 9, 12, 15, 19, 24, 30, 38}

function NovaTexture.encode(environment, width, height, pixel)
	local buffer = environment.buffer
	local bit32 = environment.bit32
	local math = environment.math
	local string = environment.string
	local stride = width * 4 + 1
	local image = buffer.create(stride * height)
	local offset = 0

	for row = 0, height - 1 do
		buffer.writeu8(image, offset, 0)
		offset += 1

		for column = 0, width - 1 do
			local red, green, blue, alpha = pixel(column, row)
			buffer.writeu8(image, offset, math.clamp(math.floor(red + 0.5), 0, 255))
			buffer.writeu8(image, offset + 1, math.clamp(math.floor(green + 0.5), 0, 255))
			buffer.writeu8(image, offset + 2, math.clamp(math.floor(blue + 0.5), 0, 255))
			buffer.writeu8(image, offset + 3, math.clamp(math.floor(alpha + 0.5), 0, 255))
			offset += 4
		end
	end

	local size = buffer.len(image)
	local first, second = 1, 0

	for index = 0, size - 1 do
		first = (first + buffer.readu8(image, index)) % 65521
		second = (second + first) % 65521
	end
